\text{mat}_{k}({\mathcal{E}_{t}})=\mathbf{A}_{e,k}\text{mat}_{k}({\mathcal{F}_{e,t}})\mathbf{A}_{e,\text{-}k}^{\prime}+\text{mat}_{k}({\bm{\Sigma}_{\epsilon}})\circ\text{mat}_{k}({\bm{\epsilon}_{t}}),

where
\mathbf{A}_{e,\text{-}k}:=\mathbf{A}_{e,K}\otimes\cdots\otimes\mathbf{A}_{e,k+1}\otimes\mathbf{A}_{e,k-1}\otimes\cdots\otimes\mathbf{A}_{e,1}
. Each mode-
k
noise fibre
\mathbf{e}_{t,k,l}
for
l\in[d_{\text{-}k}]
can then be decomposed as

\mathbf{e}_{t,k,l}:=\mathbf{A}_{e,k}\text{mat}_{k}({\mathcal{F}_{e,t}})\mathbf{A}_{e,\text{-}k,l\cdot}+\mathbf{\Sigma}_{\epsilon,k,l}^{1/2}\bm{\epsilon}_{t,k,l},

(6.3)

where
\mathbf{\Sigma}_{\epsilon,k,l}=\textnormal{diag}((\text{mat}_{k}({\bm{\Sigma}_{\epsilon}}))_{\cdot l}(\text{mat}_{k}({\bm{\Sigma}_{\epsilon}}))_{\cdot l}^{\prime})
, and
\bm{\epsilon}_{t,k,l}
contains independent elements each with mean
0
and variance
1
.

Given
h\in[d_{\text{-}k}],i\in[d_{k}]
, from (
6.3
) and Assumption (E1) and (E2), we have

\begin{split}\sum_{l\neq h}^{d_{\text{-}k}}\sum_{j=1}^{d_{k}}\Big|\mathbbm{E}[\text{mat}_{k}({\mathcal{E}_{t}})_{ih}\text{mat}_{k}({\mathcal{E}_{t}})_{jl}]\Big|&\leq\|\mathbf{A}_{e,\text{-}k,h\cdot}\|\|\mathbf{A}_{e,\text{-}k,l\cdot}\|\|\mathbf{A}_{e,k}\|_{1}\|\mathbf{A}_{e,k}\|_{\infty}=O(1).\end{split}

Moreover,

\begin{split}\sum_{j=1}^{d_{k}}\Big|\mathbbm{E}[\text{mat}_{k}({\mathcal{E}_{t}})_{ih}\text{mat}_{k}({\mathcal{E}_{t}})_{jh}]\Big|&\leq\|\text{cov}(\mathbf{e}_{t,k,h},\mathbf{e}_{t,k,h})\|_{1}\\
&\leq\|\mathbf{A}_{e,\text{-}k,h\cdot}\|^{2}\|\mathbf{A}_{e,k}\|_{1}\|\mathbf{A}_{e,k}\|_{\infty}+\|\mathbf{\Sigma}_{\epsilon,k,h}\|_{1}=O(1),\end{split}

where the last equality is from Assumption (E1).

To finish the proof of the first result in the Proposition, fix indices
k,t,i,j,h
. From Assumption (E2) and (
6.3
), we have

\begin{split}[\text{mat}_{k}({\mathcal{E}_{t}})]_{il}&=\sum_{q\geq 0}a_{e,q}\mathbf{A}_{e,k,i\cdot}^{\prime}\text{mat}_{k}({\mathcal{X}_{e,t-q}})\mathbf{A}_{e,\text{-}k,l\cdot}+[\text{mat}_{k}({\bm{\Sigma}_{\epsilon}})]_{il}\sum_{q\geq 0}a_{\epsilon,q}\text{mat}_{k}({\mathcal{X}_{\epsilon,t-q}})_{il}.\end{split}

(6.4)

Hence when
l\neq h
, from the independence between
\{\mathcal{X}_{e,t}\}
and
\{\mathcal{X}_{\epsilon,t}\}
and the independence of the elements within
\{\mathcal{X}_{\epsilon,t}\}
in Assumption (E2), we have for any
s\in\psi_{k,ij,l}
,

\begin{split}&\hskip 14.22636pt\text{cov}\Big(\text{mat}_{k}({\mathcal{E}_{t}})_{ih}\text{mat}_{k}({\mathcal{E}_{t}})_{jh},\text{mat}_{k}({\mathcal{E}_{s}})_{il}\text{mat}_{k}({\mathcal{E}_{s}})_{jl}\Big)\\
&=\text{cov}\Bigg(\Big(\sum_{q\geq 0}a_{e,q}\mathbf{A}_{e,k,i\cdot}^{\prime}\text{mat}_{k}({\mathcal{X}_{e,t-q}})\mathbf{A}_{e,\text{-}k,h\cdot}\Big)\Big(\sum_{q\geq 0}a_{e,q}\mathbf{A}_{e,k,j\cdot}^{\prime}\text{mat}_{k}({\mathcal{X}_{e,t-q}})\mathbf{A}_{e,\text{-}k,h\cdot}\Big),\\
&\Big(\sum_{q\geq 0}a_{e,q}\mathbf{A}_{e,k,i\cdot}^{\prime}\text{mat}_{k}({\mathcal{X}_{e,s-q}})\mathbf{A}_{e,\text{-}k,l\cdot}\Big)\Big(\sum_{q\geq 0}a_{e,q}\mathbf{A}_{e,k,j\cdot}^{\prime}\text{mat}_{k}({\mathcal{X}_{e,s-q}})\mathbf{A}_{e,\text{-}k,l\cdot}\Big)\Bigg).\end{split}

(6.5)

By (E2) again, all mixed covariance terms are zero except for
\text{cov}\big(\text{mat}_{k}({\mathcal{X}_{e,t-q}})_{nm}^{2},\text{mat}_{k}({\mathcal{X}_{e,t-q}})_{nm}^{2}\big)
for all
q\geq 0,n\in[r_{e,k}],m\in[r_{e,\text{-}k}]
, with coefficient

a_{e,q}^{2}a_{e,q+|t-s|}^{2}A_{e,k,in}^{2}A_{e,k,jn}^{2}A_{e,\text{-}k,hm}^{2}A_{e,\text{-}k,lm}^{2}.

Thus we have

\begin{split}&\hskip 14.22636pt\sum_{l=1,l\neq h}^{d_{\text{-}k}}\bigg|\text{cov}\Big(\text{mat}_{k}({\mathcal{E}_{t}})_{ih}\text{mat}_{k}({\mathcal{E}_{t}})_{jh},\text{mat}_{k}({\mathcal{E}_{s}})_{il}\text{mat}_{k}({\mathcal{E}_{s}})_{jl}\Big)\bigg|\\
&=\sum_{l=1,l\neq h}^{d_{\text{-}k}}\bigg|\sum_{n=1}^{r_{e,k}}\sum_{m=1}^{r_{e,\text{-}k}}\sum_{q\geq 0}a_{e,q}^{2}a_{e,q+|t-s|}^{2}A_{e,k,in}^{2}A_{e,k,jn}^{2}A_{e,\text{-}k,hm}^{2}A_{e,\text{-}k,lm}^{2}\cdot\text{cov}\big(\text{mat}_{k}({\mathcal{X}_{e,t-q}})_{nm}^{2},\text{mat}_{k}({\mathcal{X}_{e,t-q}})_{nm}^{2}\big)\bigg|\\
&=O(1)\cdot\sum_{q\geq 0}a_{e,q}^{2}a_{e,q+|t-s|}^{2}\bigg(\sum_{n=1}^{r_{e,k}}A_{e,k,in}^{2}A_{e,k,jn}^{2}\bigg)\bigg(\sum_{m=1}^{r_{e,\text{-}k}}A_{e,\text{-}k,hm}^{2}\sum_{l=1,l\neq h}^{d_{\text{-}k}}A_{e,\text{-}k,lm}^{2}\bigg)=\sum_{q\geq 0}O(a_{e,q}^{2}a_{e,q+|t-s|}^{2}),\end{split}

where we use Assumption (E2) in the second last equality, and (E1) in the last. Consequently,

\begin{split}&\hskip 14.22636pt\sum_{l=1,l\neq h}^{d_{\text{-}k}}\sum_{s\in\psi_{k,ij,l}}\bigg|\text{cov}\Big(\text{mat}_{k}({\mathcal{E}_{t}})_{ih}\text{mat}_{k}({\mathcal{E}_{t}})_{jh},\text{mat}_{k}({\mathcal{E}_{s}})_{il}\text{mat}_{k}({\mathcal{E}_{s}})_{jl}\Big)\bigg|=\sum_{q\geq 0}\sum_{s=1}^{T}O(a_{e,q}^{2}a_{e,q+|t-s|}^{2})=O(1),\end{split}

where the last equality uses Assumption (E2). Now consider lastly
l=h
.
All arguments starting from (
6.4
) follow exactly, except the following term is added in (
6.5
):

\begin{split}&\hskip 14.22636pt\sum_{q\geq 0}a_{\epsilon,q}^{2}a_{\epsilon,q+|t-s|}^{2}\Sigma_{\epsilon,k,h,ii}\Sigma_{\epsilon,k,h,jj}\cdot\text{cov}\Big(\text{mat}_{k}({\mathcal{X}_{\epsilon,t-q}})_{ih}\text{mat}_{k}({\mathcal{X}_{\epsilon,t-q}})_{jh},\text{mat}_{k}({\mathcal{X}_{\epsilon,s-q}})_{ih}\text{mat}_{k}({\mathcal{X}_{\epsilon,s-q}})_{jh}\Big)\\
&=O(1)\cdot\sum_{q\geq 0}a_{\epsilon,q}^{2}a_{\epsilon,q+|t-s|}^{2}=O(1).\end{split}

where we used again Assumptions (E2) in the last line. Finally,

\begin{split}&\hskip 14.22636pt\sum_{s\in\psi_{k,ij,h}}\bigg|\text{cov}\Big(\text{mat}_{k}({\mathcal{E}_{t}})_{ih}\text{mat}_{k}({\mathcal{E}_{t}})_{jh},\text{mat}_{k}({\mathcal{E}_{s}})_{ih}\text{mat}_{k}({\mathcal{E}_{s}})_{jh}\Big)\bigg|=O(1).\end{split}

This completes the proof of result 1 in the Proposition.

To prove the second result, fix
k\in[K],i,j\in[d_{k}]
and deterministic vectors
\mathbf{u}\in\mathbbm{R}^{r_{k}}
and
\mathbf{v}\in\mathbbm{R}^{r_{\text{-}k}}
with
\|\mathbf{u}\|,\|\mathbf{v}\|=O(1)
. Note that
\mathbbm{E}[\text{mat}_{k}({\mathcal{F}_{t}})\mathbf{v}\mathbf{v}^{\prime}\text{mat}_{k}({\mathcal{F}_{s}})^{\prime}]=\mathbf{v}^{\prime}\mathbf{v}(r_{\text{-}k}\sum_{q\geq 0}a_{f,q}a_{f,q+|t-s|})\mathbf{I}_{r_{k}}
, as the series
\{\mathcal{X}_{f,t}\}
has i.i.d. elements from Assumption (F1). Similarly, from (
6.3
) and Assumption (E1) and (E2),

\begin{split}&\hskip 14.22636pt\text{cov}(\text{mat}_{k}({\mathcal{E}_{t}})_{jh},\text{mat}_{k}({\mathcal{E}_{s}})_{jl})\\
&=\mathbbm{E}[\mathbf{A}_{e,k,j\cdot}^{\prime}\text{mat}_{k}({\mathcal{F}_{e,t}})\mathbf{A}_{e,\text{-}k,h\cdot}\mathbf{A}_{e,\text{-}k,l\cdot}^{\prime}\text{mat}_{k}({\mathcal{F}_{e,s}})^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{e,k,j\cdot}]+\mathbbm{E}[\bm{\epsilon}_{t,k,h}^{\prime}(\Sigma_{\epsilon,k,h,j\cdot}\Sigma_{\epsilon,k,l,j\cdot}^{\prime})^{1/2}\bm{\epsilon}_{s,k,l}]\\
&=\mathbf{A}_{e,\text{-}k,l\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{e,\text{-}k,h\cdot}\cdot\|\mathbf{A}_{e,k,j\cdot}\|^{2}\cdot\sum_{q\geq 0}a_{e,q}a_{e,q+|t-s|}+\mathbbm{1}_{\{h=l\}}\cdot\Sigma_{\epsilon,k,h,jj}\sum_{q\geq 0}a_{\epsilon,q}a_{\epsilon,q+|t-s|}.\end{split}

Hence if we fix
h\in[d_{\text{-}k}],t\in\psi_{k,ij,h}
, then together with Assumption (E2), we have

\begin{split}&\hskip 14.22636pt\sum_{l=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}\sum_{s\in\psi_{k,ij,l}}\frac{1}{|\psi_{k,ij,l}|}\cdot\mathbbm{E}\Big[\text{mat}_{k}({\mathcal{E}_{t}})_{jh}\mathbf{u}^{\prime}\text{mat}_{k}({\mathcal{F}_{t}})\mathbf{v}\cdot\text{mat}_{k}({\mathcal{E}_{s}})_{jl}\mathbf{v}^{\prime}\text{mat}_{k}({\mathcal{F}_{s}})^{\prime}\mathbf{u}\Big]\\
&=\sum_{l=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}\sum_{s\in\psi_{k,ij,l}}\frac{1}{|\psi_{k,ij,l}|}\cdot\text{cov}(\text{mat}_{k}({\mathcal{E}_{t}})_{jh},\text{mat}_{k}({\mathcal{E}_{s}})_{jl})\cdot\mathbbm{E}\Big[\mathbf{u}^{\prime}\text{mat}_{k}({\mathcal{F}_{t}})\mathbf{v}\mathbf{v}^{\prime}\text{mat}_{k}({\mathcal{F}_{s}})^{\prime}\mathbf{u}\Big]\\
&=\sum_{l=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}\frac{1}{|\psi_{k,ij,l}|}\bigg[O(\mathbf{A}_{e,\text{-}k,l\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{e,\text{-}k,h\cdot}\cdot\|\mathbf{A}_{e,k,j\cdot}\|^{2})\cdot\sum_{q\geq 0}\sum_{p\geq 0}\sum_{s\in\psi_{k,ij,l}}a_{e,q}a_{e,q+|t-s|}a_{f,p}a_{f,p+|t-s|}\\
&+O(\mathbbm{1}_{\{h=l\}}\cdot\Sigma_{\epsilon,k,h,jj})\cdot\sum_{q\geq 0}\sum_{p\geq 0}\sum_{s\in\psi_{k,ij,l}}a_{\epsilon,q}a_{\epsilon,q+|t-s|}a_{f,p}a_{f,p+|t-s|}\bigg]\\
&=\sum_{l=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}\frac{1}{|\psi_{k,ij,l}|}\cdot O(\mathbf{A}_{e,\text{-}k,l\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{e,\text{-}k,h\cdot}\cdot\|\mathbf{A}_{e,k,j\cdot}\|^{2}+\mathbbm{1}_{\{h=l\}}\cdot\Sigma_{\epsilon,k,h,jj})=O\Big(\frac{1}{T}\Big),\end{split}

(6.6)

where for the second last equality, we argue for the first term in the second last line only, as the second term could be shown similarly:

\begin{split}&\sum_{q\geq 0}\sum_{p\geq 0}\sum_{s\in\psi_{k,ij,h}}a_{e,q}a_{e,q+|t-s|}a_{f,p}a_{f,p+|t-s|}=\sum_{q\geq 0}\sum_{p\geq 0}a_{e,q}a_{f,p}\sum_{s\in\psi_{k,ij,h}}a_{e,q+|t-s|}a_{f,p+|t-s|}\\
&\leq\sum_{q\geq 0}\sum_{p\geq 0}|a_{e,q}|\cdot|a_{f,p}|\cdot\Bigg(\sum_{s\in\psi_{k,ij,h}}a_{e,q+|t-s|}^{2}\Bigg)^{1/2}\Bigg(\sum_{s\in\psi_{k,ij,h}}a_{f,p+|t-s|}^{2}\Bigg)^{1/2}\leq\sum_{q\geq 0}\sum_{p\geq 0}|a_{e,q}|\cdot|a_{f,p}|\leq c^{2},\end{split}

where the constant
c
is from Assumptions (F1) and (E2). Finally,

\begin{split}&\hskip 14.22636pt\mathbbm{E}\Bigg(\sum_{h=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}\sum_{t\in\psi_{k,ij,h}}\frac{1}{d_{\text{-}k}\cdot|\psi_{k,ij,h}|}\text{mat}_{k}({\mathcal{E}_{t}})_{jh}\mathbf{u}^{\prime}\text{mat}_{k}({\mathcal{F}_{t}})\mathbf{v}\Bigg)^{2}\\
&=\frac{1}{d_{\text{-}k}^{2}}\sum_{h=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}\sum_{l=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}\sum_{t\in\psi_{k,ij,h}}\sum_{s\in\psi_{k,ij,l}}\frac{1}{|\psi_{k,ij,h}|\cdot|\psi_{k,ij,l}|}\mathbbm{E}\Big[\text{mat}_{k}({\mathcal{E}_{t}})_{jh}\mathbf{u}^{\prime}\text{mat}_{k}({\mathcal{F}_{t}})\mathbf{v}\cdot\text{mat}_{k}({\mathcal{E}_{s}})_{jl}\mathbf{v}^{\prime}\text{mat}_{k}({\mathcal{F}_{s}})^{\prime}\mathbf{u}\Big]\\
&=\frac{1}{d_{\text{-}k}^{2}T}\sum_{h=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}\sum_{t\in\psi_{k,ij,h}}O\Big(\frac{1}{T}\Big)=O\Big(\frac{1}{d_{\text{-}k}T}\Big),\end{split}

which then implies result 2 of the Proposition.

Finally, we prove result 3 of the Proposition.
From Assumption (F1), we have
\mathbbm{E}[\mathcal{F}_{t}]={0}
. Next, for any
t\in[T]
, it is direct from Assumption (F1) that all elements in
\mathcal{F}_{t}
are independent. Moreover,

\begin{split}\mathbbm{E}[\text{mat}_{k}({\mathcal{F}_{t}})\text{mat}_{k}({\mathcal{F}_{t}})^{\prime}]&=\mathbbm{E}\bigg[\bigg(\sum_{q\geq 0}a_{f,q}\text{mat}_{k}({\mathcal{X}_{f,t-q}})\bigg)\bigg(\sum_{q\geq 0}a_{f,q}\text{mat}_{k}({\mathcal{X}_{f,t-q}})^{\prime}\bigg)\bigg]\\
&=\sum_{q\geq 0}a_{f,q}^{2}\mathbbm{E}[\text{mat}_{k}({\mathcal{X}_{f,t-q}})\text{mat}_{k}({\mathcal{X}_{f,t-q}})^{\prime}]=\bigg(\sum_{q\geq 0}a_{f,q}^{2}\bigg)\cdot r_{\text{-}k}\mathbf{I}_{r_{k}}=r_{\text{-}k}\mathbf{I}_{r_{k}},\end{split}

where we use Assumption (F1) in the last line. To complete the proof, WLOG consider
the variance of the
j
-th diagonal element of
\text{mat}_{k}({\mathcal{F}_{t}})\text{mat}_{k}({\mathcal{F}_{t}})^{\prime}
. From Assumption (F1), we have

\begin{split}&\text{Var}\bigg(\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}[\text{mat}_{k}({\mathcal{F}_{t}})]_{j\cdot}^{\prime}[\text{mat}_{k}({\mathcal{F}_{t}})]_{j\cdot}\bigg)=\frac{1}{T^{2}}\text{Var}\bigg(\sum_{t=1}^{T}\bigg[\sum_{q\geq 0}a_{f,q}[\text{mat}_{k}({\mathcal{X}_{f,t-q}})]_{j\cdot}^{\prime}\bigg]\bigg[\sum_{q\geq 0}a_{f,q}[\text{mat}_{k}({\mathcal{X}_{f,t-q}})]_{j\cdot}\bigg]\bigg)\\
&=\frac{1}{T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{s=1}^{T}\text{cov}\bigg(\bigg[\sum_{q\geq 0}a_{f,q}[\text{mat}_{k}({\mathcal{X}_{f,t-q}})]_{j\cdot}^{\prime}\bigg]\bigg[\sum_{q\geq 0}a_{f,q}[\text{mat}_{k}({\mathcal{X}_{f,t-q}})]_{j\cdot}\bigg],\\
&\hskip 86.72377pt\bigg[\sum_{q\geq 0}a_{f,q}[\text{mat}_{k}({\mathcal{X}_{f,s-q}})]_{j\cdot}^{\prime}\bigg]\bigg[\sum_{q\geq 0}a_{f,q}[\text{mat}_{k}({\mathcal{X}_{f,s-q}})]_{j\cdot}\bigg]\bigg)\\
&=\frac{1}{T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{q\geq 0}a_{f,q}^{4}\text{Var}([\text{mat}_{k}({\mathcal{X}_{f,t-q}})]_{j\cdot}^{\prime}[\text{mat}_{k}({\mathcal{X}_{f,t-q}})]_{j\cdot})\\
&+\frac{1}{T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{q\geq 0}\sum_{p\neq q}a_{f,q}^{2}a_{f,p}^{2}\text{Var}([\text{mat}_{k}({\mathcal{X}_{f,t-q}})]_{j\cdot}^{\prime}[\text{mat}_{k}({\mathcal{X}_{f,t-p}})]_{j\cdot})\\
&=\frac{r_{\text{-}k}}{T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{q\geq 0}a_{f,q}^{4}\text{Var}([\text{mat}_{k}({\mathcal{X}_{f,t-q}})]_{j1}^{2})+\frac{r_{\text{-}k}}{T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{q\geq 0}\sum_{p\neq q}a_{f,q}^{2}a_{f,p}^{2}\\
&=O(1)\cdot\frac{1}{T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{q\geq 0}a_{f,q}^{4}+O(1)\cdot\frac{1}{T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{q\geq 0}\sum_{p\neq q}a_{f,q}^{2}a_{f,p}^{2}=O(1)\cdot\frac{1}{T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\sum_{q\geq 0}a_{f,q}^{2})^{2}=O(\frac{1}{T})=o(1),\end{split}

where the third equality uses the independence in Assumption (E2). This completes the proof of result 3, and hence the Proposition.
\square

To prove Theorem
1
, we are going to present some lemmas and prove them first. From (
6.1
),

\begin{split}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{j\cdot}=&\widehat{\mathbf{D}}^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{d_{k}}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{i\cdot}\widehat{S}_{ij}=\widehat{\mathbf{D}}^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{d_{k}}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{i\cdot}\sum_{h=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}\frac{1}{|\psi_{ij,h}|}\sum_{t\in\psi_{ij,h}}Y_{t,ih}Y_{t,jh}.\end{split}

(6.7)

With the notations in (
6.2
), (
3.1
) can be written as
\mathbf{Y}_{t}=\mathbf{Q}\mathbf{F}_{Z,t}\bm{\Lambda}^{\prime}+\mathbf{E}_{t}
, and hence for
i,j\in[d_{k}],h\in[d_{\text{-}k}]
,

\begin{split}Y_{t,ih}=&\Big(\sum_{n=1}^{r_{k}}\sum_{m=1}^{r_{\text{-}k}}Q_{in}\Lambda_{hm}F_{Z,t,nm}\Big)+E_{t,ih}=\mathbf{Q}_{i\cdot}^{\prime}\Big(\sum_{m=1}^{r_{\text{-}k}}\Lambda_{hm}\mathbf{F}_{Z,t,\cdot m}\Big)+E_{t,ih}\\
=&\Big(\sum_{m=1}^{r_{\text{-}k}}\Lambda_{hm}\mathbf{F}_{Z,t,\cdot m}\Big)^{\prime}\mathbf{Q}_{i\cdot}+E_{t,ih}.\end{split}

Hence the product
Y_{t,ih}Y_{t,jh}
in (
6.7
) can be written as

\begin{split}Y_{t,ih}Y_{t,jh}&=\Big(\sum_{m=1}^{r_{\text{-}k}}\Lambda_{hm}\mathbf{F}_{Z,t,\cdot m}\Big)^{\prime}\mathbf{Q}_{i\cdot}\Big(\sum_{m=1}^{r_{\text{-}k}}\Lambda_{hm}\mathbf{F}_{Z,t,\cdot m}\Big)^{\prime}\mathbf{Q}_{j\cdot}+E_{t,ih}E_{t,jh}\\
&+E_{t,jh}\Big(\sum_{m=1}^{r_{\text{-}k}}\Lambda_{hm}\mathbf{F}_{Z,t,\cdot m}\Big)^{\prime}\mathbf{Q}_{i\cdot}+E_{t,ih}\Big(\sum_{m=1}^{r_{\text{-}k}}\Lambda_{hm}\mathbf{F}_{Z,t,\cdot m}\Big)^{\prime}\mathbf{Q}_{j\cdot}.\end{split}

(6.8)

We then have, from (
6.7
) and (
6.8
) that

\begin{split}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{j\cdot}-\mathbf{H}_{j}\mathbf{Q}_{j\cdot}&=\widehat{\mathbf{D}}^{-1}\Bigg(\sum_{i=1}^{d_{k}}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{i\cdot}\sum_{h=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}\frac{1}{|\psi_{ij,h}|}\sum_{t\in\psi_{ij,h}}E_{t,ih}E_{t,jh}\\
&+\sum_{i=1}^{d_{k}}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{i\cdot}\sum_{h=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}\frac{1}{|\psi_{ij,h}|}\sum_{t\in\psi_{ij,h}}E_{t,jh}\Big(\sum_{m=1}^{r_{\text{-}k}}\Lambda_{hm}\mathbf{F}_{Z,t,\cdot m}\Big)^{\prime}\mathbf{Q}_{i\cdot}\\
&+\sum_{i=1}^{d_{k}}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{i\cdot}\sum_{h=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}\frac{1}{|\psi_{ij,h}|}\sum_{t\in\psi_{ij,h}}E_{t,ih}\Big(\sum_{m=1}^{r_{\text{-}k}}\Lambda_{hm}\mathbf{F}_{Z,t,\cdot m}\Big)^{\prime}\mathbf{Q}_{j\cdot}\Bigg)\\
&=:\widehat{\mathbf{D}}^{-1}\Big(\mathcal{I}_{j}+\mathcal{II}_{j}+\mathcal{III}_{j}\Big),\end{split}

(6.9)

where

\begin{split}\mathcal{I}_{j}:&=\sum_{i=1}^{d_{k}}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{i\cdot}\sum_{h=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}\frac{1}{|\psi_{ij,h}|}\sum_{t\in\psi_{ij,h}}E_{t,ih}E_{t,jh},\\
\mathcal{II}_{j}:&=\sum_{i=1}^{d_{k}}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{i\cdot}\sum_{h=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}\frac{1}{|\psi_{ij,h}|}\sum_{t\in\psi_{ij,h}}E_{t,jh}\Big(\sum_{m=1}^{r_{\text{-}k}}\Lambda_{hm}\mathbf{F}_{Z,t,\cdot m}\Big)^{\prime}\mathbf{Q}_{i\cdot},\\
\mathcal{III}_{j}:&=\sum_{i=1}^{d_{k}}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{i\cdot}\sum_{h=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}\frac{1}{|\psi_{ij,h}|}\sum_{t\in\psi_{ij,h}}E_{t,ih}\Big(\sum_{m=1}^{r_{\text{-}k}}\Lambda_{hm}\mathbf{F}_{Z,t,\cdot m}\Big)^{\prime}\mathbf{Q}_{j\cdot}.\end{split}

The following lemma bounds the terms
\mathcal{I}_{j},\mathcal{II}_{j}
, and
\mathcal{III}_{j}
.

Lemma 1

Under Assumptions (O1), (F1), (L1), (E1) and (E2), we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{d_{k}}\sum_{j=1}^{d_{k}}\|\mathcal{I}_{j}\|_{F}^{2}

\displaystyle=O_{P}\Big(\frac{d}{T}+d_{\text{-}k}^{2}\Big),

(6.10)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{d_{k}}\sum_{j=1}^{d_{k}}\|\mathcal{II}_{j}\|_{F}^{2}

\displaystyle=O_{P}\Big(\frac{d_{\text{-}k}d_{k}^{\alpha_{k,1}}}{T}\Big)=\frac{1}{d_{k}}\sum_{j=1}^{d_{k}}\|\mathcal{III}_{j}\|_{F}^{2}.

(6.11)

Proof of Lemma
1
.

To prove (
6.10
), we decompose

\begin{split}\mathcal{I}_{j}=&\sum_{i=1}^{d_{k}}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{i\cdot}\sum_{h=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}\frac{\sum_{t\in\psi_{ij,h}}E_{t,ih}E_{t,jh}}{|\psi_{ij,h}|}\\
=&\sum_{i=1}^{d_{k}}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{i\cdot}\sum_{h=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}\Big(\frac{\sum_{t\in\psi_{ij,h}}\big(E_{t,ih}E_{t,jh}-\mathbbm{E}[E_{t,ih}E_{t,jh}]\big)}{|\psi_{ij,h}|}+\frac{\sum_{t\in\psi_{ij,h}}\mathbbm{E}[E_{t,ih}E_{t,jh}]}{|\psi_{ij,h}|}\Big)\\
=&:\sum_{i=1}^{d_{k}}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{i\cdot}\xi_{ij}+\sum_{i=1}^{d_{k}}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{i\cdot}\eta_{ij},\end{split}

(6.12)

where

\begin{split}\xi_{ij}:=&\sum_{h=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}\frac{\sum_{t\in\psi_{ij,h}}\big(E_{t,ih}E_{t,jh}-\mathbbm{E}[E_{t,ih}E_{t,jh}]\big)}{|\psi_{ij,h}|},\;\;\;\eta_{ij}:=\sum_{h=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}\frac{\sum_{t\in\psi_{ij,h}}\mathbbm{E}[E_{t,ih}E_{t,jh}]}{|\psi_{ij,h}|}.\end{split}

We want to show the following:

\displaystyle\sum_{j=1}^{d_{k}}\Big\|\sum_{i=1}^{d_{k}}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{i\cdot}\xi_{ij}\Big\|_{F}^{2}

\displaystyle=O_{P}\Big(\frac{dd_{k}}{T}\Big),

(6.13)

\displaystyle\sum_{j=1}^{d_{k}}\Big\|\sum_{i=1}^{d_{k}}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{i\cdot}\eta_{ij}\Big\|_{F}^{2}

\displaystyle=O_{P}\big(dd_{\text{-}k}\big).

(6.14)

To show (
6.13
),
first note that
\mathbbm{E}\xi_{ij}=0
, and also by Assumption (O1),

\displaystyle\mathbbm{E}|\xi_{ij}|^{2}

\displaystyle=\text{var}(\xi_{ij})\leq\frac{1}{\psi_{0}^{2}T^{2}}\text{var}\Bigg(\sum_{h=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}\sum_{t\in\psi_{ij,h}}\big(E_{t,ih}E_{t,jh}-\mathbbm{E}\big[E_{t,ih}E_{t,jh}\big]\big)\Bigg)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\psi_{0}^{2}T^{2}}\sum_{h=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}\sum_{l=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}\sum_{t\in\psi_{ij,h}}\sum_{s\in\psi_{ij,l}}\bigg|\text{cov}\Big(\big(E_{t,ih}E_{t,jh}-\mathbbm{E}\big[E_{t,ih}E_{t,jh}\big]\big),\big(E_{s,il}E_{s,jl}-\mathbbm{E}\big[E_{s,il}E_{s,jl}\big]\big)\Big)\bigg|

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\psi_{0}^{2}T^{2}}\sum_{h=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}\sum_{t\in\psi_{ij,h}}\sum_{l=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}\sum_{s\in\psi_{ij,l}}\bigg|\text{cov}\Big(E_{t,ih}E_{t,jh},E_{s,il}E_{s,jl}\Big)\bigg|\leq\frac{cd_{\text{-}k}}{\psi_{0}^{2}T},

(6.15)

where the last inequality and the constant
c
are from result 1 of Proposition
2
(hereafter Proposition
2
.1, etc).
Then by the Cauchy–Schwarz inequality,

\begin{split}\sum_{j=1}^{d_{k}}\Big\|\sum_{i=1}^{d_{k}}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{i\cdot}\xi_{ij}\Big\|_{F}^{2}\leq&\sum_{j=1}^{d_{k}}\bigg(\sum_{i=1}^{d_{k}}\big\|\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{i\cdot}\big\|_{F}^{2}\bigg)\bigg(\sum_{i=1}^{d_{k}}\xi_{ij}^{2}\bigg)=O_{P}\Big(\frac{dd_{k}}{T}\Big),\end{split}

which is (
6.13
). To show (
6.14
),
note that if we define

\begin{split}\rho_{ij,h}:&=\frac{\frac{1}{|\psi_{ij,h}|}\sum_{t\in\psi_{ij,h}}\mathbbm{E}[E_{t,ih}E_{t,jh}]}{\big(\frac{1}{|\psi_{ij,h}|}\sum_{t\in\psi_{ij,h}}\mathbbm{E}[E_{t,ih}^{2}]\big)^{1/2}\big(\frac{1}{|\psi_{ij,h}|}\sum_{t\in\psi_{ij,h}}\mathbbm{E}[E_{t,jh}^{2}]\big)^{1/2}},\end{split}

then
|\rho_{ij,h}|<1
and hence
\rho_{ij,h}^{2}\leq|\rho_{ij,h}|
. It is then easy to prove also that

\begin{split}\rho_{ij}:&=\frac{\sum_{h=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}\frac{1}{|\psi_{ij,h}|}\sum_{t\in\psi_{ij,h}}\mathbbm{E}[E_{t,ih}E_{t,jh}]}{\big(\sum_{h=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}\frac{1}{|\psi_{ij,h}|}\sum_{t\in\psi_{ij,h}}\mathbbm{E}[E_{t,ih}^{2}]\big)^{1/2}\big(\sum_{h=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}\frac{1}{|\psi_{ij,h}|}\sum_{t\in\psi_{ij,h}}\mathbbm{E}[E_{t,jh}^{2}]\big)^{1/2}},\end{split}

also satisfy
|\rho_{ij}|\leq 1
and
\rho_{ij}^{2}\leq|\rho_{ij}|
. By Proposition
2
.1,

\bigg|\sum_{h=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}\frac{\sum_{t\in\psi_{ij,h}}\mathbbm{E}[E_{t,ih}^{2}]}{|\psi_{ij,h}|}\bigg|=O_{P}(d_{\text{-}k}),

and hence

\begin{split}\eta_{ij}^{2}&=\bigg(\sum_{h=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}\frac{\sum_{t\in\psi_{ij,h}}\mathbbm{E}[E_{t,ih}E_{t,jh}]}{|\psi_{ij,h}|}\bigg)^{2}=\rho_{ij}^{2}\bigg(\sum_{h=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}\frac{\sum_{t\in\psi_{ij,h}}\mathbbm{E}[E_{t,ih}^{2}]}{|\psi_{ij,h}|}\bigg)\bigg(\sum_{h=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}\frac{\sum_{t\in\psi_{ij,h}}\mathbbm{E}[E_{t,jh}^{2}]}{|\psi_{ij,h}|}\bigg)\\
&=|\rho_{ij}|\bigg(\sum_{h=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}\frac{\sum_{t\in\psi_{ij,h}}\mathbbm{E}[E_{t,ih}^{2}]}{|\psi_{ij,h}|}\bigg)^{1/2}\bigg(\sum_{h=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}\frac{\sum_{t\in\psi_{ij,h}}\mathbbm{E}[E_{t,jh}^{2}]}{|\psi_{ij,h}|}\bigg)^{1/2}\cdot O_{P}(d_{\text{-}k})\\
&=\bigg|\sum_{h=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}\frac{\sum_{t\in\psi_{ij,h}}\mathbbm{E}[E_{t,ih}E_{t,jh}]}{|\psi_{ij,h}|}\bigg|\cdot O_{P}(d_{\text{-}k})=\big|\eta_{ij}\big|O_{P}(d_{\text{-}k}).\end{split}

Using the above, we then have

\displaystyle\sum_{j=1}^{d_{k}}\Big\|\sum_{i=1}^{d_{k}}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{i\cdot}\eta_{ij}\Big\|_{F}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{j=1}^{d_{k}}\bigg(\sum_{i=1}^{d_{k}}\big\|\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{i\cdot}\big\|_{F}^{2}\bigg)\bigg(\sum_{i=1}^{d_{k}}\eta_{ij}^{2}\bigg)\leq r_{k}\sum_{j=1}^{d_{k}}\sum_{i=1}^{d_{k}}\eta_{ij}^{2}=O_{P}(d_{\text{-}k})\cdot\sum_{j=1}^{d_{k}}\sum_{i=1}^{d_{k}}\big|\eta_{ij}\big|

\displaystyle=O_{P}(d_{\text{-}k})\cdot\frac{1}{|\psi_{0}T|}\sum_{t\in\psi_{ij,h}}\sum_{h=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}\sum_{j=1}^{d_{k}}\sum_{i=1}^{d_{k}}\Big|\mathbbm{E}[E_{t,ih}E_{t,jh}]\Big|=O_{P}(dd_{\text{-}k}),

(6.16)

where the second last equality used Assumption (O1), and the last equality used Proposition
2
.1.
This proves (
6.14
).
Using (
6.13
) and (
6.14
), from (
6.12
) we have

\begin{split}\frac{1}{d_{k}}\sum_{j=1}^{d_{k}}\big\|\mathcal{I}_{j}\big\|_{F}^{2}&=\frac{1}{d_{k}}\sum_{j=1}^{d_{k}}\Big\|\sum_{i=1}^{d_{k}}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{i\cdot}\sum_{h=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}\xi_{ij,h}+\sum_{i=1}^{d_{k}}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{i\cdot}\sum_{h=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}\eta_{ij,h}\Big\|_{F}^{2}\\
&\leq\frac{2}{d_{k}}\sum_{j=1}^{d_{k}}\Big\|\sum_{i=1}^{d_{k}}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{i\cdot}\xi_{ij}\Big\|_{F}^{2}+\frac{2}{d_{k}}\sum_{j=1}^{d_{k}}\Big\|\sum_{i=1}^{d_{k}}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{i\cdot}\eta_{ij}\Big\|_{F}^{2}=O_{P}\Big(\frac{d}{T}+d_{\text{-}k}^{2}\Big).\end{split}

This completes the proof of (
6.10
). To prove (
6.11
), consider

\begin{split}\frac{1}{d_{k}}\sum_{j=1}^{d_{k}}\|\mathcal{II}_{j}\|_{F}^{2}&=\frac{1}{d_{k}}\sum_{j=1}^{d_{k}}\bigg\|\sum_{i=1}^{d_{k}}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{i\cdot}\sum_{h=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}\frac{1}{|\psi_{ij,h}|}\sum_{t\in\psi_{ij,h}}E_{t,jh}\Big(\sum_{m=1}^{r_{\text{-}k}}\Lambda_{hm}\mathbf{F}_{Z,t,\cdot m}\Big)^{\prime}\mathbf{Q}_{i\cdot}\bigg\|_{F}^{2}\\
&\leq\frac{1}{d_{k}}\sum_{j=1}^{d_{k}}\bigg(\sum_{i=1}^{d_{k}}\|\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{i\cdot}\|_{F}^{2}\bigg)\cdot\sum_{i=1}^{d_{k}}\bigg(\sum_{h=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}\frac{1}{|\psi_{ij,h}|}\sum_{t\in\psi_{ij,h}}E_{t,jh}\Big(\sum_{m=1}^{r_{\text{-}k}}\Lambda_{hm}\mathbf{F}_{Z,t,\cdot m}\Big)^{\prime}\mathbf{Q}_{i\cdot}\bigg)^{2}\\
&=\frac{r_{k}}{d_{k}}\sum_{j=1}^{d_{k}}\sum_{i=1}^{d_{k}}\Bigg(\sum_{h=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}\frac{1}{|\psi_{ij,h}|}\sum_{t\in\psi_{ij,h}}E_{t,jh}\Big(\sum_{m=1}^{r_{\text{-}k}}\Lambda_{hm}\mathbf{F}_{Z,t,\cdot m}\Big)^{\prime}\mathbf{Q}_{i\cdot}\Bigg)^{2}\\
&=\frac{r_{k}d_{\text{-}k}^{2}}{d_{k}}\sum_{j=1}^{d_{k}}\sum_{i=1}^{d_{k}}\Bigg(\sum_{h=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}\sum_{t\in\psi_{ij,h}}\frac{1}{d_{\text{-}k}\cdot|\psi_{ij,h}|}E_{t,jh}[\otimes_{l\in[K]\setminus\{k\}}\mathbf{A}_{l}]_{h\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{i\cdot}\Bigg)^{2}\\
&=\frac{r_{k}d_{\text{-}k}^{2}}{d_{k}}\sum_{j=1}^{d_{k}}\sum_{i=1}^{d_{k}}\|\mathbf{u}_{i}\|_{F}^{2}\Bigg(\sum_{h=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}\sum_{t\in\psi_{ij,h}}\frac{1}{d_{\text{-}k}\cdot|\psi_{ij,h}|}E_{t,jh}\mathbf{v}_{h}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\frac{1}{\|\mathbf{u}_{i}\|_{F}}\mathbf{u}_{i}\Bigg)^{2},\end{split}

(6.17)

where
\mathbf{A}=\mathbf{A}_{k}
and
\mathbf{F}_{t}\ =\text{mat}_{k}({\mathcal{F}_{t}})
above, and we define

\mathbf{v}_{h}:=[\otimes_{l\in[K]\setminus\{k\}}\mathbf{A}_{l}]_{h\cdot},\;\mathbf{u}_{i}:=\mathbf{A}_{i\cdot}.

By Proposition
2
.2, the last bracket in the last line of (
6.17
) is
O_{P}(d_{\text{-}k}^{-1}T^{-1})
, and hence

\begin{split}\frac{1}{d_{k}}\sum_{j=1}^{d_{k}}\|\mathcal{II}_{j}\|_{F}^{2}&=O_{P}(\frac{d_{\text{-}k}}{d_{k}T})\sum_{j=1}^{d_{k}}\sum_{i=1}^{d_{k}}\|\mathbf{u}_{i}\|_{F}^{2}=O_{P}\Big(\frac{d_{\text{-}k}}{d_{k}T}\Big)\sum_{j=1}^{d_{k}}\big\|\mathbf{A}\big\|_{F}^{2}=O_{P}\Big(\frac{d_{\text{-}k}d_{k}^{\alpha_{k,1}}}{T}\Big),\end{split}

where the last equality follows since for any
l\in[K],\|\mathbf{A}_{l}\|_{F}^{2}=O_{P}\big(\textnormal{tr}(\mathbf{Z}_{l})\big)=O_{P}\big(d_{l}^{\alpha_{l,1}}\big)
by Assumption (L1). The bound corresponding to
\mathcal{III}_{j}
can be proved similarly (omitted), and hence (
6.11
) is established. This concludes the proof of Lemma
1
.

\square

Lemma 2

Under Assumptions (O1), (M1), (F1), (L1), (E1), (E2) and (R1), with
\mathbf{H}_{j}
and
\widehat{\mathbf{D}}
from (
6.2
), we have

\displaystyle\big\|\widehat{\mathbf{D}}^{-1}\big\|_{F}

\displaystyle=O_{P}\Bigg(d_{k}^{\alpha_{k,1}-\alpha_{k,r_{k}}}\prod_{j=1}^{K}d_{j}^{-\alpha_{j,1}}\Bigg),

(6.18)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{d_{k}}\sum_{j=1}^{d_{k}}\Big\|\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{j\cdot}-\mathbf{H}_{j}\mathbf{Q}_{j\cdot}\Big\|_{F}^{2}

\displaystyle=O_{P}\Bigg(d_{k}^{2(\alpha_{k,1}-\alpha_{k,r_{k}})-1}\bigg(\frac{1}{Td_{\text{-}k}}+\frac{1}{d_{k}}\bigg)\prod_{j=1}^{K}d_{j}^{2(1-\alpha_{j,1})}\Bigg).

(6.19)

Proof of Lemma
2
.

First, we bound the term
\big\|\widehat{\mathbf{D}}^{-1}\big\|_{F}^{2}
by finding the lower bound of
\lambda_{r_{k}}(\widehat{\mathbf{D}})
. To do this, define
\omega_{k}:=d_{k}^{\alpha_{k,r_{k}}-\alpha_{k,1}}\prod_{j=1}^{K}d_{j}^{\alpha_{j,1}}
, and consider the decomposition

\displaystyle\widehat{\mathbf{S}}

\displaystyle=\mathbf{R}^{*}+(\widetilde{\mathbf{R}}-\mathbf{R}^{*})+\mathbf{R}_{1}+\mathbf{R}_{2}+\mathbf{R}_{3},

(6.20)

where for a unit vector
\bm{\gamma}
,

\displaystyle R(\bm{\gamma})

\displaystyle:=\frac{1}{\omega_{k}}\bm{\gamma}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{S}}\bm{\gamma}=\frac{1}{\omega_{k}}\sum_{i=1}^{d_{k}}\sum_{j=1}^{d_{k}}\bm{\gamma}_{i}\bm{\gamma}_{j}\widehat{S}_{ij}=\frac{1}{\omega_{k}}\sum_{i=1}^{d_{k}}\sum_{j=1}^{d_{k}}\bm{\gamma}_{i}\bm{\gamma}_{j}\sum_{h=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}\frac{1}{|\psi_{ij,h}|}\sum_{t\in\psi_{ij,h}}Y_{t,ih}Y_{t,jh}

\displaystyle=:R^{*}(\bm{\gamma})+(\widetilde{R}(\bm{\gamma})-R^{*}(\bm{\gamma}))+R_{1}+R_{2}+R_{3},\;\text{ with}

\displaystyle\widetilde{R}(\bm{\gamma})

\displaystyle:=\frac{1}{\omega_{k}}\bm{\gamma}^{\prime}\widetilde{\mathbf{R}}\bm{\gamma}:=\frac{1}{\omega_{k}}\sum_{i=1}^{d_{k}}\sum_{j=1}^{d_{k}}\bm{\gamma}_{i}\bm{\gamma}_{j}\sum_{h=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}\frac{1}{|\psi_{ij,h}|}\sum_{t\in\psi_{ij,h}}\Big(\sum_{m=1}^{r_{\text{-}k}}\Lambda_{hm}\mathbf{F}_{Z,t,\cdot m}\Big)^{\prime}\mathbf{Q}_{i\cdot}\Big(\sum_{m=1}^{r_{\text{-}k}}\Lambda_{hm}\mathbf{F}_{Z,t,\cdot m}\Big)^{\prime}\mathbf{Q}_{j\cdot},

\displaystyle R^{*}(\bm{\gamma})

\displaystyle:=\frac{1}{\omega_{k}}\bm{\gamma}^{\prime}\mathbf{R}^{*}\bm{\gamma}:=\frac{1}{\omega_{k}}\sum_{i=1}^{d_{k}}\sum_{j=1}^{d_{k}}\bm{\gamma}_{i}\bm{\gamma}_{j}\sum_{h=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\Big(\sum_{m=1}^{r_{\text{-}k}}\Lambda_{hm}\mathbf{F}_{Z,t,\cdot m}\Big)^{\prime}\mathbf{Q}_{i\cdot}\Big(\sum_{m=1}^{r_{\text{-}k}}\Lambda_{hm}\mathbf{F}_{Z,t,\cdot m}\Big)^{\prime}\mathbf{Q}_{j\cdot},

\displaystyle R_{1}

\displaystyle:=\frac{1}{\omega_{k}}\bm{\gamma}^{\prime}\mathbf{R}_{1}\bm{\gamma}:=\frac{1}{\omega_{k}}\sum_{i=1}^{d_{k}}\sum_{j=1}^{d_{k}}\bm{\gamma}_{i}\bm{\gamma}_{j}\sum_{h=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}\frac{1}{|\psi_{ij,h}|}\sum_{t\in\psi_{ij,h}}E_{t,ih}E_{t,jh},

\displaystyle R_{2}

\displaystyle:=\frac{1}{\omega_{k}}\bm{\gamma}^{\prime}\mathbf{R}_{2}\bm{\gamma}:=\frac{1}{\omega_{k}}\sum_{i=1}^{d_{k}}\sum_{j=1}^{d_{k}}\bm{\gamma}_{i}\bm{\gamma}_{j}\sum_{h=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}\frac{1}{|\psi_{ij,h}|}\sum_{t\in\psi_{ij,h}}E_{t,jh}\Big(\sum_{m=1}^{r_{\text{-}k}}\Lambda_{hm}\mathbf{F}_{Z,t,\cdot m}\Big)^{\prime}\mathbf{Q}_{i\cdot},

\displaystyle R_{3}

\displaystyle:=\frac{1}{\omega_{k}}\bm{\gamma}^{\prime}\mathbf{R}_{3}\bm{\gamma}:=\frac{1}{\omega_{k}}\sum_{i=1}^{d_{k}}\sum_{j=1}^{d_{k}}\bm{\gamma}_{i}\bm{\gamma}_{j}\sum_{h=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}\frac{1}{|\psi_{ij,h}|}\sum_{t\in\psi_{ij,h}}E_{t,ih}\Big(\sum_{m=1}^{r_{\text{-}k}}\Lambda_{hm}\mathbf{F}_{Z,t,\cdot m}\Big)^{\prime}\mathbf{Q}_{j\cdot},

(6.21)

and we used (
6.8
) for the expansion above.
Then we have the decomposition

\begin{split}R(\bm{\gamma})-R^{\ast}(\bm{\gamma})=R(\bm{\gamma})-\tilde{R}(\bm{\gamma})+\tilde{R}(\bm{\gamma})-R^{\ast}(\bm{\gamma}).\end{split}

(6.22)

Similar to the treatment of the term
\mathcal{I}_{j}
in the proof of Lemma
1
, since
\|\bm{\gamma}\|=1
,

\displaystyle|R_{1}|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\omega_{k}}\bigg|\sum_{i=1}^{d_{k}}\sum_{j=1}^{d_{k}}\bm{\gamma}_{i}\bm{\gamma}_{j}\xi_{ij}\bigg|+\frac{1}{\omega_{k}}\bigg|\sum_{i=1}^{d_{k}}\sum_{j=1}^{d_{k}}\bm{\gamma}_{i}\bm{\gamma}_{j}\eta_{ij}\bigg|\leq\frac{1}{\omega_{k}}\bigg(\sum_{i=1}^{d_{k}}\sum_{j=1}^{d_{k}}\xi_{ij}^{2}\bigg)^{1/2}+\frac{1}{\omega_{k}}\bigg(\sum_{i=1}^{d_{k}}\sum_{j=1}^{d_{k}}\eta_{ij}^{2}\bigg)^{1/2}

\displaystyle=O_{P}\bigg(d_{k}^{\alpha_{k,1}-\alpha_{k,r_{k}}}\Big[\frac{1}{T^{1/2}d_{\text{-}k}^{1/2}}+\frac{1}{d_{k}^{1/2}}\Big]\prod_{j=1}^{K}d_{j}^{1-\alpha_{j,1}}\bigg)=O_{P}(d[(Td_{\text{-}k})^{-1/2}+d_{k}^{-1/2}]/\omega_{k}),

(6.23)

where the second last equality is from (
6.15
) and part of (
6.16
).
Together with Assumption (R1), (
6.23
) implies that as
T,d_{k},d_{\text{-}k}\to\infty
,
R_{1}\xrightarrow{p}0
.

From (
6.17
) and the arguments for
\mathcal{II}_{j}
immediately afterwards, we see that

\displaystyle|R_{2}|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\omega_{k}}\Bigg\{\sum_{j=1}^{d_{k}}\sum_{i=1}^{d_{k}}\Bigg(\sum_{h=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}\frac{1}{|\psi_{ij,h}|}\sum_{t\in\psi_{ij,h}}E_{t,jh}\Big(\sum_{m=1}^{r_{\text{-}k}}\Lambda_{hm}\mathbf{F}_{Z,t,\cdot m}\Big)^{\prime}\mathbf{Q}_{i\cdot}\Bigg)^{2}\Bigg\}^{1/2}

\displaystyle=O_{P}(\omega_{k}^{-1})\cdot O_{P}\bigg(T^{-1/2}d^{1/2}\prod_{j=1}^{K}d_{j}^{\alpha_{j,1}/2}\bigg)=O_{P}((dg_{s})^{1/2}T^{-1/2}/\omega_{k})=o_{P}(1),

(6.24)

where the last equality is from Assumption (R1). The term
R_{3}
can be proved to have the same rate with same lines of proof as for
R_{2}
. Hence we have

\sup_{\|\bm{\gamma}\|=1}|R(\bm{\gamma})-\widetilde{R}(\bm{\gamma})|=\sup_{\|\bm{\gamma}\|=1}|R_{1}+R_{2}+R_{3}|\xrightarrow{p}0.

(6.25)

Similar to the proof of (R6) in Lemma 4 in
Xiong and Pelger, 2023
, using the definition
\mathbf{v}_{h}:=[\otimes_{l\in[K]\setminus\{k\}}\mathbf{A}_{l}]_{h\cdot}
as before,

\displaystyle\tilde{R}(\bm{\gamma})-R^{\ast}(\bm{\gamma})

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\omega_{k}}\sum_{i=1}^{d_{k}}\sum_{j=1}^{d_{k}}\bm{\gamma}_{i}\bm{\gamma}_{j}\sum_{h=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}\bigg(\frac{1}{|\psi_{ij,h}|}\sum_{t\in\psi_{ij,h}}\mathbf{Q}_{i\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{Z,t}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{h\cdot}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{h\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{Z,t}^{\prime}\mathbf{Q}_{j\cdot}-\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{Q}_{i\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{Z,t}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{h\cdot}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{h\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{Z,t}^{\prime}\mathbf{Q}_{j\cdot}\bigg)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\omega_{k}}\sum_{i=1}^{d_{k}}\sum_{j=1}^{d_{k}}\bm{\gamma}_{i}\bm{\gamma}_{j}\sum_{h=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}\mathbf{A}_{i\cdot}^{\prime}\bigg(\frac{1}{|\psi_{ij,h}|}\sum_{t\in\psi_{ij,h}}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{v}_{h}\mathbf{v}_{h}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}-\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{v}_{h}\mathbf{v}_{h}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\bigg)\mathbf{A}_{j\cdot}

\displaystyle=:\frac{1}{\omega_{k}}\sum_{i=1}^{d_{k}}\sum_{j=1}^{d_{k}}\bm{\gamma}_{i}\bm{\gamma}_{j}\sum_{h=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}\mathbf{A}_{i\cdot}^{\prime}\Delta_{F,k,ij,h}\mathbf{A}_{j\cdot},\;\text{ where }

(6.26)

\displaystyle\Delta_{F,k,ij,h}

\displaystyle:=\frac{1}{|\psi_{ij,h}|}\sum_{t\in\psi_{ij,h}}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{v}_{h}\mathbf{v}_{h}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}-\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{v}_{h}\mathbf{v}_{h}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}.

By the Cauchy–Schwarz inequality, we then have

\begin{split}\big|\tilde{R}(\bm{\gamma})-R^{\ast}(\bm{\gamma})\big|&\leq\frac{1}{\omega_{k}}\Bigg(\sum_{i=1}^{d_{k}}\sum_{j=1}^{d_{k}}\Big[\mathbf{A}_{i\cdot}^{\prime}\Big(\sum_{h=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}\Delta_{F,k,ij,h}\Big)\mathbf{A}_{j\cdot}\Big]^{2}\Bigg)^{1/2}.\end{split}

(6.27)

With Assumption (M1), using the standard rate of convergence in the weak law of large number for
\alpha
-mixing sequence and the fact that the elements in
\mathcal{F}_{t}
are independent from Assumption (F1), since
\Delta_{F,k,ij,h}
has fixed dimension, we have for each
k\in[K],i,j\in[d_{k}]
and
h\in[d_{\text{-}k}]
,

\displaystyle\|\Delta_{F,k,ij,h}\|_{F}\leq\bigg\|\frac{1}{|\psi_{ij,h}|}\sum_{t\in\psi_{ij,h}}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{v}_{h}\mathbf{v}_{h}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}-\mathbf{v}_{h}^{\prime}\mathbf{v}_{h}\bm{\Sigma}_{k}\bigg\|_{F}+\bigg\|\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{v}_{h}\mathbf{v}_{h}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}-\mathbf{v}_{h}^{\prime}\mathbf{v}_{h}\bm{\Sigma}_{k}\bigg\|_{F}=O_{P}\Big(\frac{\|\mathbf{v}_{h}\|^{2}}{T^{1/2}}\Big).

(6.28)

From Assumption (L1), we then have

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{d_{k}}\sum_{j=1}^{d_{k}}\Big[\mathbf{A}_{i\cdot}^{\prime}\Big(\sum_{h=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}\Delta_{F,k,ij,h}\Big)\mathbf{A}_{j\cdot}\Big]^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{i=1}^{d_{k}}\sum_{j=1}^{d_{k}}\|\mathbf{A}_{i\cdot}\|_{F}^{2}\|\mathbf{A}_{j\cdot}\|_{F}^{2}\Big(\sum_{h=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}\|\Delta_{F,k,ij,h}\|_{F}\Big)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\|\mathbf{A}_{k}\|_{F}^{4}\cdot O_{P}\bigg(\frac{1}{T}\Big(\sum_{h=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}\|\mathbf{v}_{h}\|^{2}\Big)^{2}\bigg)=\|\mathbf{A}_{k}\|_{F}^{4}\cdot O_{P}\bigg(\frac{1}{T}\|\otimes_{j\in[K]\setminus\{k\}}\mathbf{A}_{j}\|_{F}^{4}\bigg)

\displaystyle=O_{P}\bigg(\frac{1}{T}\prod_{j=1}^{K}\big\|\mathbf{A}_{j}\big\|_{F}^{4}\bigg)=O_{P}\bigg(\frac{1}{T}\prod_{j=1}^{K}d_{j}^{2\alpha_{j,1}}\bigg),

(6.29)

and hence from (
6.27
), we have by Assumption (R1) that

\displaystyle\big|\tilde{R}(\bm{\gamma})-R^{\ast}(\bm{\gamma})\big|=O_{P}\Big(\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}d_{k}^{\alpha_{k,1}-\alpha_{k,r_{k}}}\Big)=o_{P}(d^{-1/2}g_{s}^{1/2})=o_{P}(1),

(6.30)

where the second last equality is from Assumption (R1).
Next, with Proposition
2
.3, consider

\displaystyle\lambda_{r_{k}}(\mathbf{R}^{*})

\displaystyle=\lambda_{r_{k}}\Bigg(\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{Q}\mathbf{F}_{Z,t}\mathbf{\Lambda}^{\prime}\mathbf{\Lambda}\mathbf{F}_{Z,t}^{\prime}\mathbf{Q}^{\prime}\Bigg)=\lambda_{r_{k}}\Bigg(\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{A}_{k}\mathbf{F}_{t}\big[\otimes_{j\in[K]\setminus\{k\}}\mathbf{A}_{j}\big]^{\prime}\big[\otimes_{j\in[K]\setminus\{k\}}\mathbf{A}_{j}\big]\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{k}^{\prime}\Bigg)

\displaystyle\geq\lambda_{r_{k}}(\mathbf{A}_{k}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{k})\cdot\lambda_{r_{k}}\Bigg(\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}\big[\otimes_{j\in[K]\setminus\{k\}}\mathbf{A}_{j}\big]^{\prime}\big[\otimes_{j\in[K]\setminus\{k\}}\mathbf{A}_{j}\big]\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\Bigg)

\displaystyle\asymp_{P}d_{k}^{\alpha_{k,r_{k}}}\cdot\lambda_{r_{k}}(\textnormal{tr}(\otimes_{j\in[K]\setminus\{k\}}\mathbf{A}_{j}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{j})\bm{\Sigma}_{k})\asymp_{P}d_{k}^{\alpha_{k,r_{k}}}\prod_{j\in[K]\setminus\{k\}}d_{j}^{\alpha_{j,1}}=\omega_{k}.

With this, going back to the decomposition (
6.20
),

\displaystyle\omega_{k}^{-1}\lambda_{r_{k}}(\widehat{\mathbf{D}})

\displaystyle=\omega_{k}^{-1}\lambda_{r_{k}}(\widehat{\mathbf{S}})\geq\omega_{k}^{-1}\lambda_{r_{k}}(\mathbf{R}^{*})-\sup_{\|\bm{\gamma}\|=1}|\widetilde{R}(\bm{\gamma})-R^{*}(\bm{\gamma})|-\sup_{\|\bm{\gamma}\|=1}|R_{1}+R_{2}+R_{3}|\asymp_{P}1,

where we used (
6.25
) and (
6.30
). Hence finally,

\displaystyle\big\|\widehat{\mathbf{D}}^{-1}\big\|_{F}=O_{P}\big(\lambda_{r_{k}}^{-1}(\widehat{\mathbf{D}})\big)=O_{P}(\omega_{k}^{-1})=O_{P}\bigg(d_{k}^{\alpha_{k,1}-\alpha_{k,r_{k}}}\prod_{j=1}^{K}d_{j}^{-\alpha_{j,1}}\bigg),

which completes the proof of (
6.18
).

To prove (
6.19
),
from (
6.9
) we obtain

\begin{split}\frac{1}{d_{k}}\sum_{j=1}^{d_{k}}\Big\|\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{j\cdot}-\mathbf{H}_{j}\mathbf{Q}_{j\cdot}\Big\|_{F}^{2}&=\frac{1}{d_{k}}\sum_{j=1}^{d_{k}}\Big\|\widehat{\mathbf{D}}^{-1}\Big(\mathcal{I}_{j}+\mathcal{II}_{j}+\mathcal{III}_{j}\Big)\Big\|_{F}^{2}\leq\big\|\widehat{\mathbf{D}}^{-1}\big\|_{F}^{2}\Bigg[\frac{1}{d_{k}}\sum_{j=1}^{d_{k}}\Big\|\mathcal{I}_{j}+\mathcal{II}_{j}+\mathcal{III}_{j}\Big\|_{F}^{2}\Bigg]\\
&\leq\big\|\widehat{\mathbf{D}}^{-1}\big\|_{F}^{2}\Bigg[\bigg(\frac{2}{d_{k}}\sum_{j=1}^{d_{k}}\big\|\mathcal{I}_{j}\big\|_{F}^{2}\bigg)+\bigg(\frac{4}{d_{k}}\sum_{j=1}^{d_{k}}\big\|\mathcal{II}_{j}\big\|_{F}^{2}\bigg)+\bigg(\frac{4}{d_{k}}\sum_{j=1}^{d_{k}}\big\|\mathcal{III}_{j}\big\|_{F}^{2}\bigg)\Bigg]\\
&=O_{P}\Bigg(d_{k}^{2(\alpha_{k,1}-\alpha_{k,r_{k}})-1}\bigg(\frac{1}{Td_{\text{-}k}}+\frac{1}{d_{k}}\bigg)\prod_{j=1}^{K}d_{j}^{2(1-\alpha_{j,1})}\Bigg),\end{split}

where the last line follows from (
6.18
) and Lemma
1
. This concludes the proof of Lemma
2
.

\square

Lemma 3

Under the Assumptions in Lemma
2
, for each
i,j\in[d_{k}]
and
h\in[d_{\text{-}k}]
, if the set
\psi_{ij,h}
has cardinality satisfying

\frac{|\psi_{ij,h}|}{T}=1-\eta_{i,j,h}\geq 1-\eta,

then for all
j\in[d_{k}]
with
\mathbf{H}_{j}
and
\mathbf{H}^{a}
from (
6.2
),

\big\|\mathbf{H}_{j}-\mathbf{H}^{a}\big\|_{F}^{2}=O_{P}\bigg(\min\bigg(\frac{1}{T},\frac{\eta^{2}}{(1-\eta)^{2}}\bigg)d_{k}^{2(\alpha_{k,1}-\alpha_{k,r_{k}})}\bigg)=o_{P}(1).

(6.31)

Proof of Lemma
3
.

Firstly, consider
\Delta_{F,k,ij,h}
from (
6.26
), where

\displaystyle\|\Delta_{F,k,ij,h}\|_{F}

\displaystyle=\bigg\|\frac{1}{|\psi_{ij,h}|}\sum_{t\in\psi_{ij,h}}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{v}_{h}\mathbf{v}_{h}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}-\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{v}_{h}\mathbf{v}_{h}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\bigg\|_{F}

\displaystyle=\bigg\|\frac{1}{|\psi_{ij,h}|}\sum_{t\in\psi_{ij,h}^{c}}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{v}_{h}\mathbf{v}_{h}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\bigg\|_{F}+\bigg\|\bigg(\frac{1}{|\psi_{ij,h}|}-\frac{1}{T}\bigg)\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{v}_{h}\mathbf{v}_{h}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\bigg\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq O_{P}\bigg(\frac{T\eta}{T-T\eta}\big\|\mathbf{v}_{h}\big\|^{2}\bigg)+\frac{T\eta}{T(T-T\eta)}\cdot O_{P}(T\big\|\mathbf{v}_{h}\big\|^{2})

\displaystyle=O_{P}\bigg(\frac{\eta}{1-\eta}\big\|\mathbf{v}_{h}\big\|^{2}\bigg).

Combining this with (
6.28
), we have

\|\Delta_{F,k,ij,h}\|_{F}=O_{P}\bigg(\min\bigg(\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}},\frac{\eta}{1-\eta}\bigg)\big\|\mathbf{v}_{h}\big\|^{2}\bigg).

(6.32)

Note also that

\begin{split}\mathbf{H}_{j}=&\widehat{\mathbf{D}}^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{d_{k}}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{i\cdot}\sum_{h=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}\frac{1}{|\psi_{ij,h}|}\sum_{t\in\psi_{ij,h}}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{h\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{Z,t}^{\prime}\mathbf{Q}_{i\cdot}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{h\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{Z,t}^{\prime}\\
=&\widehat{\mathbf{D}}^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{d_{k}}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{i\cdot}\sum_{h=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}\frac{1}{|\psi_{ij,h}|}\sum_{t\in\psi_{ij,h}}\mathbf{Q}_{i\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{Z,t}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{h\cdot}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{h\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{Z,t}^{\prime},\end{split}

and also

\begin{split}\mathbf{H}^{a}=&\widehat{\mathbf{D}}^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{d_{k}}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{i\cdot}\sum_{h=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{Q}_{i\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{Z,t}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{h\cdot}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{h\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{Z,t}^{\prime}.\end{split}

We then have, for
\widehat{\mathbf{A}}_{i\cdot}=\mathbf{Z}_{k}^{1/2}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{i\cdot}
and using (
6.32
),

\begin{split}\big\|\mathbf{H}_{j}-\mathbf{H}^{a}\big\|_{F}^{2}&=\bigg\|\widehat{\mathbf{D}}^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{d_{k}}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{i\cdot}\sum_{h=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}\Big(\frac{1}{|\psi_{ij,h}|}\sum_{t\in\psi_{ij,h}}\mathbf{Q}_{i\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{Z,t}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{h\cdot}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{h\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{Z,t}^{\prime}-\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{Q}_{i\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{Z,t}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{h\cdot}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{h\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{Z,t}^{\prime}\Big)\bigg\|_{F}^{2}\\
&=\bigg\|\widehat{\mathbf{D}}^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{d_{k}}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{i\cdot}\mathbf{A}_{i\cdot}^{\prime}\sum_{h=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}\Delta_{F,k,ij,h}\mathbf{Z}_{k}^{1/2}\bigg\|_{F}^{2}\leq\big\|\widehat{\mathbf{D}}^{-1}\big\|_{F}^{2}\bigg\|\sum_{i=1}^{d_{k}}\widehat{\mathbf{A}}_{i\cdot}\mathbf{A}_{i\cdot}^{\prime}\sum_{h=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}\Delta_{F,k,ij,h}\bigg\|_{F}^{2}\\
&=O_{P}(\omega_{k}^{-2})\cdot\max_{i\in[d_{k}]}\bigg\|\sum_{h=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}\Delta_{F,k,ij,h}\bigg\|_{F}^{2}\bigg(\sum_{i=1}^{d_{k}}\|\widehat{\mathbf{A}}_{i\cdot}\|\cdot\|\mathbf{A}_{i\cdot}\|\bigg)^{2}\\
&=O_{P}(\omega_{k}^{-2})\cdot O_{P}\bigg(\min\bigg(\frac{1}{T},\frac{\eta^{2}}{(1-\eta)^{2}}\bigg)\|\otimes_{j\in[K]\setminus\{k\}}\mathbf{A}_{j}\|_{F}^{4}\bigg)\cdot\big\|\widehat{\mathbf{A}}_{k}\big\|_{F}^{2}\cdot\|\mathbf{A}_{k}\|_{F}^{2}\\
&=O_{P}\bigg(\min\bigg(\frac{1}{T},\frac{\eta^{2}}{(1-\eta)^{2}}\bigg)d_{k}^{2(\alpha_{k,1}-\alpha_{k,r_{k}})}\bigg)=o_{P}(1),\end{split}

where we used (
6.32
) in the second last line, Assumption (L1) in the second last equality, and Assumption (R1) in the last equality.
This completes the proof of Lemma
3
.
\square

Proof of Theorem
1
.

The first result is shown in Lemma
2
. Together with Lemma
3
, we have

\begin{split}\frac{1}{d_{k}}\sum_{j=1}^{d_{k}}\big\|\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{j\cdot}-\mathbf{H}^{a}\mathbf{Q}_{j\cdot}\big\|^{2}&\leq\frac{1}{d_{k}}\sum_{j=1}^{d_{k}}\big\|\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{j\cdot}-\mathbf{H}_{j}\mathbf{Q}_{j\cdot}\big\|_{F}^{2}+\frac{1}{d_{k}}\sum_{j=1}^{d_{k}}\Big\|\Big(\mathbf{H}_{j}-\mathbf{H}^{a}\Big)\mathbf{Q}_{j\cdot}\Big\|_{F}^{2}\\
&\leq\frac{1}{d_{k}}\sum_{j=1}^{d_{k}}\big\|\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{j\cdot}-\mathbf{H}_{j}\mathbf{Q}_{j\cdot}\big\|_{F}^{2}+\frac{1}{d_{k}}\sum_{j=1}^{d_{k}}\big\|\mathbf{H}_{j}-\mathbf{H}^{a}\big\|_{F}^{2}\big\|\mathbf{Q}_{j\cdot}\big\|_{F}^{2}\\
&=O_{P}\Bigg(d_{k}^{2(\alpha_{k,1}-\alpha_{k,r_{k}})-1}\bigg(\frac{1}{Td_{\text{-}k}}+\frac{1}{d_{k}}\bigg)\prod_{j=1}^{K}d_{j}^{2(1-\alpha_{j,1})}\Bigg)\\
&\;\;\;+O_{P}\bigg(\min\bigg(\frac{1}{T},\frac{\eta^{2}}{(1-\eta)^{2}}\bigg)d_{k}^{2(\alpha_{k,1}-\alpha_{k,r_{k}})-1}\bigg)\\
&=O_{P}\Bigg(d_{k}^{2(\alpha_{k,1}-\alpha_{k,r_{k}})-1}\Bigg[\bigg(\frac{1}{Td_{\text{-}k}}+\frac{1}{d_{k}}\bigg)\frac{d^{2}}{g_{s}^{2}}+\min\bigg(\frac{1}{T},\frac{\eta^{2}}{(1-\eta)^{2}}\bigg)\Bigg]\Bigg),\end{split}

where we used
d_{k}^{-1}\sum_{j=1}^{d_{k}}\|\mathbf{Q}_{j\cdot}\|_{F}^{2}=d_{k}^{-1}\|\mathbf{Q}_{k}\|_{F}^{2}=O(d_{k}^{-1})
by Assumption (L1). This completes the proof of the theorem.
\square

Before we prove the consistency results for our imputations, we want to prove asymptotic normality for our factor loading estimators first. Consistency for the imputations will then use the rate obtained from asymptotic normality of the estimated factor loading matrices. We present a lemma first before proving Theorem
3
.

Lemma 4

Let Assumption (O1), (M1), (F1), (L1), (L2), (E1), (E2) and (R1) hold. For a given
k\in[K]
, let
\mathbf{R}^{\ast}
be from (
6.20
) and
\omega_{k}:=d_{k}^{\alpha_{k,r_{k}}-\alpha_{k,1}}g_{s}
. Then

\displaystyle\omega_{k}^{-1}\mathbf{R}^{\ast}

\displaystyle\xrightarrow{p}\textnormal{tr}(\mathbf{A}_{\text{-}k}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{\text{-}k})\cdot\omega_{k}^{-1}\mathbf{A}_{k}\mathbf{A}_{k}^{\prime},

\displaystyle\omega_{k}^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{D}}_{k}

\displaystyle\xrightarrow{p}\omega_{k}^{-1}\mathbf{D}_{k}:=\omega_{k}^{-1}\textnormal{tr}(\mathbf{A}_{\text{-}k}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{\text{-}k})\cdot\textnormal{diag}\{\lambda_{j}(\mathbf{A}_{k}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{k})\mid j\in[r_{k}]\},

\displaystyle\mathbf{H}_{k}^{a}

\displaystyle\xrightarrow{p}\mathbf{H}_{k}^{a,\ast}:=(\textnormal{tr}(\mathbf{A}_{\text{-}k}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{\text{-}k}))^{1/2}\cdot\mathbf{D}_{k}^{-1/2}\Upsilon_{k}^{\prime}\mathbf{Z}_{k}^{1/2},

where
\Upsilon_{k}
is the eigenvector matrix of
\textnormal{tr}(\mathbf{A}_{\text{-}k}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{\text{-}k})\cdot\omega_{k}^{-1}\mathbf{Z}_{k}^{1/2}\bm{\Sigma}_{A,k}\mathbf{Z}_{k}^{1/2}
.

Proof of Lemma
4
.

First, let
\widehat{\mathbf{S}},\widehat{\mathbf{Q}},\widehat{\mathbf{D}},\mathbf{H}^{a}
be from (
6.2
), and
\mathbf{R}^{\ast},\widetilde{\mathbf{R}},\mathbf{R}_{1},\mathbf{R}_{2},\mathbf{R}_{3}
from (
6.20
). Define also
\mathbf{H}^{a,\ast}:=\mathbf{H}_{k}^{a,\ast}
, then we have

\begin{split}\frac{1}{\omega_{k}}(\widehat{\mathbf{D}}-\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}^{\prime}\mathbf{R}^{\ast}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}})&=\frac{1}{\omega_{k}}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}^{\prime}(\widehat{\mathbf{S}}-\mathbf{R}^{\ast})\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}\\
&=\frac{1}{\omega_{k}}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}^{\prime}(\widetilde{\mathbf{R}}-\mathbf{R}^{\ast})\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}+\frac{1}{\omega_{k}}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}^{\prime}\mathbf{R}_{1}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}+\frac{1}{\omega_{k}}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}^{\prime}\mathbf{R}_{2}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}+\frac{1}{\omega_{k}}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}^{\prime}\mathbf{R}_{3}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}=o_{P}(1),\end{split}

(6.33)

where the last equality follows from the proof of Lemma
2
.

Using the structure in Assumption (F1), we have

\begin{split}\mathbbm{E}\Big[\frac{1}{\omega_{k}}\mathbf{R}^{\ast}\Big]&=\frac{1}{\omega_{k}T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbbm{E}\bigg[\mathbf{A}_{k}\Big(\sum_{q\geq 0}a_{f,q}\mathbf{X}_{f,t-q}\Big)\mathbf{A}_{\text{-}k}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{\text{-}k}\Big(\sum_{q\geq 0}a_{f,q}\mathbf{X}_{f,t-q}^{\prime}\Big)\mathbf{A}_{k}^{\prime}\bigg]\\
&=\textnormal{tr}(\mathbf{A}_{\text{-}k}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{\text{-}k})\cdot\frac{1}{\omega_{k}T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{q\geq 0}a_{f,q}^{2}\mathbf{A}_{k}\mathbf{A}_{k}^{\prime}=\textnormal{tr}(\mathbf{A}_{\text{-}k}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{\text{-}k})\cdot\frac{1}{\omega_{k}}\mathbf{A}_{k}\mathbf{A}_{k}^{\prime}.\end{split}

Meanwhile, we have

\begin{split}\text{Var}\Big(\Big(\frac{1}{\omega_{k}}\mathbf{R}^{\ast}\Big)_{ij}\Big)&=\frac{1}{\omega_{k}^{2}T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{s=1}^{T}\text{cov}\bigg(\mathbf{A}_{k,i\cdot}^{\prime}\Big(\sum_{q\geq 0}a_{f,q}\mathbf{X}_{f,t-q}\Big)\mathbf{A}_{\text{-}k}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{\text{-}k}\Big(\sum_{q\geq 0}a_{f,q}\mathbf{X}_{f,t-q}^{\prime}\Big)\mathbf{A}_{k,j\cdot},\\
&\mathbf{A}_{k,i\cdot}^{\prime}\Big(\sum_{q\geq 0}a_{f,q}\mathbf{X}_{f,s-q}\Big)\mathbf{A}_{\text{-}k}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{\text{-}k}\Big(\sum_{q\geq 0}a_{f,q}\mathbf{X}_{f,s-q}^{\prime}\Big)\mathbf{A}_{k,j\cdot}\bigg)\\
&=\frac{1}{\omega_{k}^{2}T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{q\geq 0}\sum_{p\geq 0}a_{f,q}^{2}a_{f,p}^{2}\cdot\text{Var}\bigg(\mathbf{A}_{k,i\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{X}_{f,t-q}\mathbf{A}_{\text{-}k}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{\text{-}k}\mathbf{X}_{f,t-p}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{k,j\cdot}\bigg)\\
&=\frac{1}{\omega_{k}^{2}T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{q\geq 0}\sum_{p\geq 0}a_{f,q}^{2}a_{f,p}^{2}\cdot O_{P}(\|\mathbf{A}_{\text{-}k}\|_{F}^{2})\\
&=O_{P}\Bigg(d_{k}^{-2\alpha_{k,r_{k}}}\frac{1}{T}\prod_{j\in[K]\setminus\{k\}}d_{j}^{-\alpha_{j,1}}\Bigg)=o_{P}(1),\end{split}

where we used Assumption (E2) in the third last equality, both (L1) and (F1) in the second last, and (R1) in the last. We can then conclude
\omega_{k}^{-1}\mathbf{R}^{\ast}\xrightarrow{p}\textnormal{tr}(\mathbf{A}_{\text{-}k}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{\text{-}k})\cdot\omega_{k}^{-1}\mathbf{A}_{k}\mathbf{A}_{k}^{\prime}
. Together with (
6.33
) and Assumption (L2), we obtain the limit of
\omega_{k}^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{D}}
as

\omega_{k}^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{D}}\xrightarrow{p}\omega_{k}^{-1}\mathbf{D}=\omega_{k}^{-1}\textnormal{tr}(\mathbf{A}_{\text{-}k}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{\text{-}k})\cdot\textnormal{diag}\{\lambda_{j}(\mathbf{A}_{k}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{k})\mid j\in[r_{k}]\}.

(6.34)

Define further

\mathbf{R}_{\text{res}}:=\omega_{k}^{-1}\mathbf{Z}_{k}^{1/2}\mathbf{Q}^{\prime}((\widetilde{\mathbf{R}}-\mathbf{R}^{\ast})+\mathbf{R}_{1}+\mathbf{R}_{2}+\mathbf{R}_{3})\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}.

With similar arguments in the proof of Lemma
2
, we have
\|\mathbf{R}_{\text{res}}\|_{F}=o_{P}(\|\mathbf{Z}_{k}^{1/2}\|_{F})
. Left-multiply both sides of
\widehat{\mathbf{S}}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}=\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}\widehat{\mathbf{D}}
by
\omega_{k}^{-1}\mathbf{Z}_{k}^{1/2}\mathbf{Q}^{\prime}
, we can write

\begin{split}(\mathbf{Z}_{k}^{1/2}\mathbf{Q}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}})(\omega_{k}^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{D}})&=\omega_{k}^{-1}\mathbf{Z}_{k}^{1/2}\mathbf{Q}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{S}}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}=\omega_{k}^{-1}\mathbf{Z}_{k}^{1/2}\mathbf{Q}^{\prime}\mathbf{R}^{\ast}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}+\mathbf{R}_{\text{res}}\\
&=\Big[\omega_{k}^{-1}\mathbf{Z}_{k}^{1/2}\mathbf{Q}^{\prime}\mathbf{R}^{\ast}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}(\mathbf{Z}_{k}^{1/2}\mathbf{Q}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}})^{-1}+\mathbf{R}_{\text{res}}(\mathbf{Z}_{k}^{1/2}\mathbf{Q}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}})^{-1}\Big](\mathbf{Z}_{k}^{1/2}\mathbf{Q}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}).\end{split}

Hence, each column of
\mathbf{Z}_{k}^{1/2}\mathbf{Q}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}
is an eigenvector of the matrix

\omega_{k}^{-1}\mathbf{Z}_{k}^{1/2}\mathbf{Q}^{\prime}\mathbf{R}^{\ast}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}(\mathbf{Z}_{k}^{1/2}\mathbf{Q}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}})^{-1}+\mathbf{R}_{\text{res}}(\mathbf{Z}_{k}^{1/2}\mathbf{Q}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}})^{-1}.

We have
(\mathbf{Z}_{k}^{1/2}\mathbf{Q}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}})^{\prime}(\mathbf{Z}_{k}^{1/2}\mathbf{Q}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}})\xrightarrow{p}(\textnormal{tr}(\mathbf{A}_{\text{-}k}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{\text{-}k}))^{-1}\cdot\mathbf{D}
, since

\begin{split}&\hskip 14.22636pt\omega_{k}^{-1}\Big[(\mathbf{Z}_{k}^{1/2}\mathbf{Q}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}})^{\prime}(\mathbf{Z}_{k}^{1/2}\mathbf{Q}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}})-(\textnormal{tr}(\mathbf{A}_{\text{-}k}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{\text{-}k}))^{-1}\cdot\mathbf{D}\Big]\\
&=[\omega_{k}^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{k}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{k}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}-\omega_{k}^{-1}(\textnormal{tr}(\mathbf{A}_{\text{-}k}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{\text{-}k}))^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}^{\prime}\mathbf{R}^{\ast}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}]+[\omega_{k}^{-1}(\textnormal{tr}(\mathbf{A}_{\text{-}k}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{\text{-}k}))^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}^{\prime}\mathbf{R}^{\ast}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}-\omega_{k}^{-1}(\textnormal{tr}(\mathbf{A}_{\text{-}k}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{\text{-}k}))^{-1}\cdot\mathbf{D}],\end{split}

which is
o_{P}(1)
from the limit of
\omega_{k}^{-1}\mathbf{R}^{\ast}
(for the first square bracket) and from (
6.33
) and (
6.34
) (for the second square bracket).
Hence the eigenvalues of
(\mathbf{Q}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}})^{\prime}(\mathbf{Q}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}})
are asymptotically bounded away from zero and infinity by Assumption (L1), and also
\|(\mathbf{Z}_{k}^{1/2}\mathbf{Q}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}})^{-1}\|_{F}=O_{P}(\|\mathbf{Z}_{k}^{-1/2}\|_{F})
.
Let

\Upsilon_{k}^{\ast}:=(\textnormal{tr}(\mathbf{A}_{\text{-}k}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{\text{-}k}))^{1/2}\cdot(\mathbf{Z}_{k}^{1/2}\mathbf{Q}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}})\mathbf{D}^{-1/2}.

Using the limit of
\omega_{k}^{-1}\mathbf{R}^{\ast}
, we have

\begin{split}\omega_{k}^{-1}\mathbf{Z}_{k}^{1/2}\mathbf{Q}^{\prime}\mathbf{R}^{\ast}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}(\mathbf{Z}_{k}^{1/2}\mathbf{Q}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}})^{-1}&\xrightarrow{p}\textnormal{tr}(\mathbf{A}_{\text{-}k}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{\text{-}k})\cdot\omega_{k}^{-1}\mathbf{Z}_{k}^{1/2}\mathbf{Q}^{\prime}\mathbf{Q}\mathbf{Z}_{k}\mathbf{Q}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}(\mathbf{Q}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}})^{-1}\mathbf{Z}_{k}^{-1/2}\\
&=\textnormal{tr}(\mathbf{A}_{\text{-}k}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{\text{-}k})\cdot\omega_{k}^{-1}\mathbf{Z}_{k}^{1/2}\mathbf{Q}^{\prime}\mathbf{Q}\mathbf{Z}_{k}^{1/2}\\
&=\textnormal{tr}(\mathbf{A}_{\text{-}k}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{\text{-}k})\cdot\omega_{k}^{-1}\mathbf{Z}_{k}^{1/2}\bm{\Sigma}_{A,k}\mathbf{Z}_{k}^{1/2},\end{split}

and
\|\mathbf{R}_{\text{res}}(\mathbf{Z}_{k}^{1/2}\mathbf{Q}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}})^{-1}\|_{F}=o_{P}(1)
from the above. By Assumption (L2) and eigenvector perturbation theories, there exists a unique eigenvector matrix
\Upsilon_{k}
of
\textnormal{tr}(\mathbf{A}_{\text{-}k}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{\text{-}k})\cdot\omega_{k}^{-1}\mathbf{Z}_{k}^{1/2}\bm{\Sigma}_{A,k}\mathbf{Z}_{k}^{1/2}
such that
\|\Upsilon_{k}-\Upsilon_{k}^{\ast}\|=o_{P}(1)
. Therefore, we have

\mathbf{Q}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}=(\textnormal{tr}(\mathbf{A}_{\text{-}k}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{\text{-}k}))^{-1/2}\cdot\mathbf{Z}_{k}^{-1/2}\Upsilon_{k}^{\ast}\mathbf{D}^{1/2}\xrightarrow{p}(\textnormal{tr}(\mathbf{A}_{\text{-}k}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{\text{-}k}))^{-1/2}\cdot\mathbf{Z}_{k}^{-1/2}\Upsilon_{k}\mathbf{D}^{1/2}.

Thus, we have

\begin{split}\mathbf{H}^{a}&=\widehat{\mathbf{D}}^{-1}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{k}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{A}_{\text{-}k}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{\text{-}k}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{Z}_{k}^{1/2}=\widehat{\mathbf{D}}^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}^{\prime}\mathbf{R}^{\ast}\mathbf{A}_{k}(\mathbf{A}_{k}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{k})^{-1}\mathbf{Z}_{k}^{1/2}=\widehat{\mathbf{D}}^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}^{\prime}\mathbf{R}^{\ast}\mathbf{Q}\bm{\Sigma}_{A,k}^{-1}\\
&=\textnormal{tr}(\mathbf{A}_{\text{-}k}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{\text{-}k})\cdot\mathbf{D}^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}^{\prime}\mathbf{Q}\mathbf{Z}_{k}\mathbf{Q}^{\prime}\mathbf{Q}\bm{\Sigma}_{A,k}^{-1}+o_{P}(1)\\
&\xrightarrow{p}(\textnormal{tr}(\mathbf{A}_{\text{-}k}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{\text{-}k}))^{1/2}\cdot\mathbf{D}^{-1/2}\Upsilon_{k}^{\prime}\mathbf{Z}_{k}^{1/2}.\end{split}

(6.35)

This completes the proof of Lemma
4
.

\square

Proof of Theorem
3
.

Suppose we focus on the
k
-th mode, and hence we adapt all notations by omitting the subscript
k
for the ease of notational simplicity; see (
6.2
) for example. Moreover, we set
\mathbf{X}_{e,t}:=\text{mat}_{k}({\mathcal{X}_{e,t}}),\mathbf{X}_{\epsilon,t}:=\text{mat}_{k}({\mathcal{X}_{\epsilon,t}})
and
\mathbf{X}_{f,t}:=\text{mat}_{k}({\mathcal{X}_{f,t}})
.

To proceed, we first decompose

\begin{split}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{j\cdot}-\mathbf{H}^{a}\mathbf{Q}_{j\cdot}=(\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{j\cdot}-\mathbf{H}_{j}\mathbf{Q}_{j\cdot})+(\mathbf{H}_{j}-\mathbf{H}^{a})\mathbf{Q}_{j\cdot}.\end{split}

(6.36)

Consider the first term
(\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{j\cdot}-\mathbf{H}_{j}\mathbf{Q}_{j\cdot})
. Using the decomposition in (
6.9
),

\begin{split}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{j\cdot}-\mathbf{H}_{j}\mathbf{Q}_{j\cdot}&=\widehat{\mathbf{D}}^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{d_{k}}(\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{i\cdot}-\mathbf{H}_{j}\mathbf{Q}_{i\cdot})\sum_{h=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}\frac{1}{|\psi_{ij,h}|}\sum_{t\in\psi_{ij,h}}E_{t,jh}(\mathbf{A}_{\text{-}k})_{h\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{i\cdot}\\
&+\widehat{\mathbf{D}}^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{d_{k}}(\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{i\cdot}-\mathbf{H}_{j}\mathbf{Q}_{i\cdot})\sum_{h=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}\frac{1}{|\psi_{ij,h}|}\sum_{t\in\psi_{ij,h}}E_{t,ih}(\mathbf{A}_{\text{-}k})_{h\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{j\cdot}\\
&+\widehat{\mathbf{D}}^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{d_{k}}(\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{i\cdot}-\mathbf{H}_{j}\mathbf{Q}_{i\cdot})\sum_{h=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}\frac{1}{|\psi_{ij,h}|}\sum_{t\in\psi_{ij,h}}E_{t,ih}E_{t,jh}\\
&+\mathcal{I}_{H,j}+\mathcal{I}_{H,j}^{\ast}+\mathcal{II}_{H,j}+\mathcal{III}_{H,j},\;\text{ where }\end{split}

(6.37)

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{H,j}

\displaystyle:=\widehat{\mathbf{D}}^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{d_{k}}\mathbf{H}^{a}\mathbf{Q}_{i\cdot}\sum_{h=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}\frac{1}{|\psi_{ij,h}|}\sum_{t\in\psi_{ij,h}}E_{t,jh}(\mathbf{A}_{\text{-}k})_{h\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{i\cdot},

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{H,j}^{\ast}

\displaystyle:=\widehat{\mathbf{D}}^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{d_{k}}(\mathbf{H}_{j}-\mathbf{H}^{a})\mathbf{Q}_{i\cdot}\sum_{h=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}\frac{1}{|\psi_{ij,h}|}\sum_{t\in\psi_{ij,h}}E_{t,jh}(\mathbf{A}_{\text{-}k})_{h\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{i\cdot},

\displaystyle\mathcal{II}_{H,j}

\displaystyle:=\widehat{\mathbf{D}}^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{d_{k}}\mathbf{H}_{j}\mathbf{Q}_{i\cdot}\sum_{h=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}\frac{1}{|\psi_{ij,h}|}\sum_{t\in\psi_{ij,h}}E_{t,ih}(\mathbf{A}_{\text{-}k})_{h\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{j\cdot},

\displaystyle\mathcal{III}_{H,j}

\displaystyle:=\widehat{\mathbf{D}}^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{d_{k}}\mathbf{H}_{j}\mathbf{Q}_{i\cdot}\sum_{h=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}\frac{1}{|\psi_{ij,h}|}\sum_{t\in\psi_{ij,h}}E_{t,ih}E_{t,jh},\;\text{ with }\mathbf{A}_{\text{-}k}:=\otimes_{l\in[K]\setminus\{k\}}\mathbf{A}_{l}.

We want to show that
\mathcal{I}_{H,j}
is the leading term among those in (
6.37
).
Equivalently, we will compare the eight terms below, and show that first term has lower bound dominating the upper bound of others:

\displaystyle\bigg\|\sum_{i=1}^{d_{k}}\mathbf{H}^{a}\mathbf{Q}_{i\cdot}\sum_{h=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}\frac{1}{|\psi_{ij,h}|}\sum_{t\in\psi_{ij,h}}E_{t,jh}(\mathbf{A}_{\text{-}k})_{h\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{i\cdot}\bigg\|^{2},

(6.38)

\displaystyle\bigg\|\sum_{i=1}^{d_{k}}(\mathbf{H}_{j}-\mathbf{H}^{a})\mathbf{Q}_{i\cdot}\sum_{h=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}\frac{1}{|\psi_{ij,h}|}\sum_{t\in\psi_{ij,h}}E_{t,jh}(\mathbf{A}_{\text{-}k})_{h\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{i\cdot}\bigg\|^{2},

(6.39)

\displaystyle\bigg\|\sum_{i=1}^{d_{k}}\mathbf{H}_{j}\mathbf{Q}_{i\cdot}\sum_{h=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}\frac{1}{|\psi_{ij,h}|}\sum_{t\in\psi_{ij,h}}E_{t,ih}(\mathbf{A}_{\text{-}k})_{h\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{j\cdot}\bigg\|^{2},

(6.40)

\displaystyle\bigg\|\sum_{i=1}^{d_{k}}\mathbf{H}_{j}\mathbf{Q}_{i\cdot}\sum_{h=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}\frac{1}{|\psi_{ij,h}|}\sum_{t\in\psi_{ij,h}}E_{t,ih}E_{t,jh}\bigg\|^{2},

(6.41)

\displaystyle\bigg\|\sum_{i=1}^{d_{k}}(\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{i\cdot}-\mathbf{H}_{j}\mathbf{Q}_{i\cdot})\sum_{h=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}\frac{1}{|\psi_{ij,h}|}\sum_{t\in\psi_{ij,h}}E_{t,jh}(\mathbf{A}_{\text{-}k})_{h\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{i\cdot}\bigg\|^{2},

(6.42)

\displaystyle\bigg\|\sum_{i=1}^{d_{k}}(\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{i\cdot}-\mathbf{H}_{j}\mathbf{Q}_{i\cdot})\sum_{h=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}\frac{1}{|\psi_{ij,h}|}\sum_{t\in\psi_{ij,h}}E_{t,ih}(\mathbf{A}_{\text{-}k})_{h\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{j\cdot}\bigg\|^{2},

(6.43)

\displaystyle\bigg\|\sum_{i=1}^{d_{k}}(\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{i\cdot}-\mathbf{H}_{j}\mathbf{Q}_{i\cdot})\sum_{h=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}\frac{1}{|\psi_{ij,h}|}\sum_{t\in\psi_{ij,h}}E_{t,ih}E_{t,jh}\bigg\|^{2}.

(6.44)

Note that we can easily see the rates of (
6.40
) and (
6.41
) are greater than those of (
6.43
) and (
6.44
) respectively, using Lemma
2
and the Cauchy–Schwarz inequality.

Consider (
6.39
) first. We have

\begin{split}&\hskip 14.22636pt\bigg\|\sum_{i=1}^{d_{k}}(\mathbf{H}_{j}-\mathbf{H}^{a})\mathbf{Q}_{i\cdot}\sum_{h=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}\frac{1}{|\psi_{ij,h}|}\sum_{t\in\psi_{ij,h}}E_{t,jh}(\mathbf{A}_{\text{-}k})_{h\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{i\cdot}\bigg\|^{2}\\
&\leq\bigg(\sum_{i=1}^{d_{k}}\|\mathbf{H}_{j}-\mathbf{H}^{a}\|_{F}^{2}\cdot\|\mathbf{Q}_{i\cdot}\|^{2}\bigg)\cdot\sum_{i=1}^{d_{k}}\bigg(\sum_{h=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}\frac{1}{|\psi_{ij,h}|}\sum_{t\in\psi_{ij,h}}E_{t,jh}(\mathbf{A}_{\text{-}k})_{h\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{i\cdot}\bigg)^{2}\\
&=O(d_{\text{-}k}^{2})\cdot\|\mathbf{H}_{j}-\mathbf{H}^{a}\|_{F}^{2}\cdot\sum_{i=1}^{d_{k}}\bigg(\sum_{h=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}\sum_{t\in\psi_{ij,h}}\frac{1}{d_{\text{-}k}\cdot|\psi_{ij,h}|}E_{t,jh}(\mathbf{A}_{\text{-}k})_{h\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{i\cdot}\bigg)^{2}\\
&=O(d_{\text{-}k}^{2})\cdot\|\mathbf{H}_{j}-\mathbf{H}^{a}\|_{F}^{2}\cdot\sum_{i=1}^{d_{k}}\|\mathbf{u}_{i}\|^{2}\bigg(\sum_{h=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}\sum_{t\in\psi_{ij,h}}\frac{1}{d_{\text{-}k}\cdot|\psi_{ij,h}|}E_{t,jh}\mathbf{v}_{h}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\frac{1}{\|\mathbf{u}_{i}\|}\mathbf{u}_{i}\bigg)^{2}\\
&=O_{P}\Bigg(d_{k}^{2(\alpha_{k,1}-\alpha_{k,r_{k}})}\Big(\frac{d_{\text{-}k}}{T^{2}}\Big)d_{k}^{\alpha_{k,1}}\Bigg),\end{split}

where
\mathbf{v}_{h}:=(\mathbf{A}_{\text{-}k})_{h\cdot},\;\mathbf{u}_{i}:=\mathbf{A}_{i\cdot}
, and we used Lemma
3
, Proposition
2
.2 and (L1) in the last equality.

To bound (
6.40
), note from Assumption (E1) and (E2) that we can write

E_{t,ih}=\sum_{q\geq 0}a_{e,q}\mathbf{A}_{e,k,i\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{X}_{e,t-q}\mathbf{A}_{e,\text{-}k,h\cdot}+[\text{mat}_{k}({\bm{\Sigma}_{\epsilon}})]_{ih}\sum_{q\geq 0}a_{\epsilon,q}(\mathbf{X}_{\epsilon,t-q})_{ih}.

Consider first
\sum_{h=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}\sum_{t\in\psi_{ij,h}}(\sum_{q\geq 0}a_{e,q}\mathbf{A}_{e,k,i\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{X}_{e,t-q}\mathbf{A}_{e,\text{-}k,h\cdot})(\mathbf{A}_{\text{-}k})_{h\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{j\cdot}
. By Assumption (O1), (E1), (E2) and (F1), we have

\begin{split}&\hskip 14.22636pt\mathbbm{E}\Big[\big(\sum_{h=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}\sum_{t\in\psi_{ij,h}}(\sum_{q\geq 0}a_{e,q}\mathbf{A}_{e,k,i\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{X}_{e,t-q}\mathbf{A}_{e,\text{-}k,h\cdot})(\mathbf{A}_{\text{-}k})_{h\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{j\cdot}\big)^{2}\Big]\\
&=\text{cov}\Big(\sum_{h=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}\sum_{t\in\psi_{ij,h}}(\mathbf{A}_{\text{-}k})_{h\cdot}^{\prime}\big(\sum_{q\geq 0}a_{f,q}\mathbf{X}_{f,t-q}^{\prime}\big)\mathbf{A}_{j\cdot}\big(\sum_{q\geq 0}a_{e,q}\mathbf{A}_{e,k,i\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{X}_{e,t-q}\mathbf{A}_{e,\text{-}k,h\cdot}\big),\\
&\sum_{h=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}\sum_{t\in\psi_{ij,h}}(\mathbf{A}_{\text{-}k})_{h\cdot}^{\prime}\big(\sum_{q\geq 0}a_{f,q}\mathbf{X}_{f,t-q}^{\prime}\big)\mathbf{A}_{j\cdot}\big(\sum_{q\geq 0}a_{e,q}\mathbf{A}_{e,k,i\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{X}_{e,t-q}\mathbf{A}_{e,\text{-}k,h\cdot}\big)\Big)\\
&=\sum_{h=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}\sum_{l=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}\sum_{t\in\psi_{ij,h}\cap\psi_{ij,l}}\sum_{q\geq 0}a_{f,q}^{2}a_{e,q}^{2}\cdot\|\mathbf{A}_{j\cdot}\|^{2}\cdot\|(\mathbf{A}_{\text{-}k})_{h\cdot}\|\cdot\|(\mathbf{A}_{\text{-}k})_{l\cdot}\|\cdot\|\mathbf{A}_{e,\text{-}k,h\cdot}\|\cdot\|\mathbf{A}_{e,\text{-}k,l\cdot}\|\cdot\|\mathbf{A}_{e,k,i\cdot}\|^{2}\\
&=O(T)\cdot\|\mathbf{A}_{j\cdot}\|^{2}\cdot\|\mathbf{A}_{e,k,i\cdot}\|^{2}.\end{split}

(6.45)

Consider also
\sum_{i=1}^{d_{k}}\sum_{h=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}\sum_{t\in\psi_{ij,h}}\mathbf{Q}_{i\cdot}([\text{mat}_{k}({\bm{\Sigma}_{\epsilon}})]_{ih}\sum_{q\geq 0}a_{\epsilon,q}(\mathbf{X}_{\epsilon,t-q})_{ih})(\mathbf{A}_{\text{-}k})_{h\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{j\cdot}
. Similarly, by Assumption (O1), (E1), (E2) and (F1), we have

\begin{split}&\hskip 14.22636pt\mathbbm{E}\Big[\big\|\sum_{i=1}^{d_{k}}\sum_{h=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}\sum_{t\in\psi_{ij,h}}\mathbf{Q}_{i\cdot}([\text{mat}_{k}({\bm{\Sigma}_{\epsilon}})]_{ih}\sum_{q\geq 0}a_{\epsilon,q}(\mathbf{X}_{\epsilon,t-q})_{ih})(\mathbf{A}_{\text{-}k})_{h\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{j\cdot}\big\|^{2}\Big]\\
&=\text{cov}\Big(\sum_{i=1}^{d_{k}}\sum_{h=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}\sum_{t\in\psi_{ij,h}}\mathbf{Q}_{i\cdot}(\mathbf{A}_{\text{-}k})_{h\cdot}^{\prime}\big(\sum_{q\geq 0}a_{f,q}\mathbf{X}_{f,t-q}^{\prime}\big)\mathbf{A}_{j\cdot}\big([\text{mat}_{k}({\bm{\Sigma}_{\epsilon}})]_{ih}\sum_{q\geq 0}a_{\epsilon,q}(\mathbf{X}_{\epsilon,t-q})_{ih}\big),\\
&\sum_{i=1}^{d_{k}}\sum_{h=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}\sum_{t\in\psi_{ij,h}}\mathbf{Q}_{i\cdot}(\mathbf{A}_{\text{-}k})_{h\cdot}^{\prime}\big(\sum_{q\geq 0}a_{f,q}\mathbf{X}_{f,t-q}^{\prime}\big)\mathbf{A}_{j\cdot}\big([\text{mat}_{k}({\bm{\Sigma}_{\epsilon}})]_{ih}\sum_{q\geq 0}a_{\epsilon,q}(\mathbf{X}_{\epsilon,t-q})_{ih}\big)\Big)\\
&=\sum_{i=1}^{d_{k}}\sum_{h=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}\sum_{t\in\psi_{ij,h}}\sum_{q\geq 0}a_{f,q}^{2}a_{\epsilon,q}^{2}\cdot\|\mathbf{A}_{j\cdot}\|^{2}\cdot\|(\mathbf{A}_{\text{-}k})_{h\cdot}\|^{2}\cdot\Sigma_{\epsilon,k,h,ii}\cdot\|\mathbf{Q}_{i\cdot}\|^{2}\\
&=O(T)\cdot\|\mathbf{A}_{j\cdot}\|^{2}\cdot\|\mathbf{A}_{\text{-}k}\|^{2}\cdot\|\mathbf{Q}\|^{2}.\end{split}

(6.46)

Hence it holds that

\begin{split}&\hskip 14.22636pt\bigg\|\sum_{i=1}^{d_{k}}\mathbf{H}_{j}\mathbf{Q}_{i\cdot}\sum_{h=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}\frac{1}{|\psi_{ij,h}|}\sum_{t\in\psi_{ij,h}}E_{t,ih}(\mathbf{A}_{\text{-}k})_{h\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{j\cdot}\bigg\|^{2}\\
&\leq\|\mathbf{H}_{j}\|_{F}^{2}\cdot\bigg\|\sum_{i=1}^{d_{k}}\sum_{h=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}\frac{1}{|\psi_{ij,h}|}\sum_{t\in\psi_{ij,h}}\mathbf{Q}_{i\cdot}([\text{mat}_{k}({\bm{\Sigma}_{\epsilon}})]_{ih}\sum_{q\geq 0}a_{\epsilon,q}(\mathbf{X}_{\epsilon,t-q})_{ih})(\mathbf{A}_{\text{-}k})_{h\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{j\cdot}\bigg\|^{2}\\
&+\|\mathbf{H}_{j}\|_{F}^{2}\cdot\bigg(\sum_{i=1}^{d_{k}}\|\mathbf{Q}_{i\cdot}\|^{2}\bigg)\cdot\sum_{i=1}^{d_{k}}\bigg\|\sum_{h=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}\frac{1}{|\psi_{ij,h}|}\sum_{t\in\psi_{ij,h}}(\sum_{q\geq 0}a_{e,q}\mathbf{A}_{e,k,i\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{X}_{e,t-q}\mathbf{A}_{e,\text{-}k,h\cdot})(\mathbf{A}_{\text{-}k})_{h\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{j\cdot}\bigg\|^{2}\\
&=O_{P}\Bigg(d_{k}^{-\alpha_{k,1}}\bigg(\frac{1}{T}\bigg)\prod_{j=1}^{K}d_{j}^{\alpha_{j,1}}\Bigg),\end{split}

where we used Assumption (L1), (
6.45
) and (
6.46
) in the last equality.

For (
6.41
), by Assumption (O1), (E1) and (E2), we have from the proof of Proposition
2
that

\begin{split}&\hskip 14.22636pt\text{Var}\Big(\sum_{i=1}^{d_{k}}\sum_{h=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}\sum_{t\in\psi_{ij,h}}\mathbf{Q}_{i\cdot}E_{t,ih}E_{t,jh}\Big)\\
&=O(1)\cdot\sum_{i=1}^{d_{k}}\sum_{w=1}^{d_{k}}\sum_{h=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}\sum_{l=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}\sum_{t\in\psi_{ij,h}\cap\psi_{wj,l}}\sum_{n=1}^{r_{e,k}}\sum_{m=1}^{r_{e,\text{-}k}}\sum_{q\geq 0}a_{e,q}^{4}A_{e,k,in}A_{e,k,wn}A_{e,k,jn}^{2}A_{e,\text{-}k,hm}^{2}A_{e,\text{-}k,lm}^{2}\\
&\cdot\|\mathbf{Q}_{i\cdot}\|\cdot\|\mathbf{Q}_{w\cdot}\|\cdot\text{Var}((\mathbf{X}_{e,t-q})_{nm}^{2})\\
&+O(1)\cdot\sum_{i=1}^{d_{k}}\sum_{h=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}\sum_{t\in\psi_{ij,h}\cap\psi_{wj,l}}\sum_{q\geq 0}a_{\epsilon,q}^{4}\Sigma_{\epsilon,k,h,ii}\Sigma_{\epsilon,k,h,jj}\cdot\|\mathbf{Q}_{i\cdot}\|^{2}\cdot\text{Var}((\mathbf{X}_{\epsilon,t-q})_{ih}(\mathbf{X}_{\epsilon,t-q})_{jh})\\
&=O(T+Td_{\text{-}k})=O(Td_{\text{-}k}).\end{split}

Moreover, it holds that

\begin{split}\mathbbm{E}\Big[\sum_{i=1}^{d_{k}}\sum_{h=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}\sum_{t\in\psi_{ij,h}}E_{t,ih}E_{t,jh}\Big]&=\sum_{i=1}^{d_{k}}\sum_{h=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}\sum_{t\in\psi_{ij,h}}\bigg(\|\mathbf{A}_{e,\text{-}k,h\cdot}\|^{2}\cdot\|\mathbf{A}_{e,k,i\cdot}\|\cdot\|\mathbf{A}_{e,k,j\cdot}\|+\Sigma_{\epsilon,k,h,ij}\bigg)=O(Td_{\text{-}k}),\end{split}

and with
\max_{i}\|\mathbf{Q}_{i\cdot}\|^{2}\leq\|\mathbf{A}_{j\cdot}\|^{2}\cdot\|\mathbf{Z}_{k}^{-1/2}\|^{2}=O_{P}\big(d_{k}^{-\alpha_{k,r_{k}}}\big)
, we thus have

\begin{split}\bigg\|\sum_{i=1}^{d_{k}}\mathbf{H}_{j}\mathbf{Q}_{i\cdot}\sum_{h=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}\frac{1}{|\psi_{ij,h}|}\sum_{t\in\psi_{ij,h}}E_{t,ih}E_{t,jh}\bigg\|^{2}&\leq\|\mathbf{H}_{j}\|_{F}^{2}\cdot\bigg\|\sum_{i=1}^{d_{k}}\mathbf{Q}_{i\cdot}\sum_{h=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}\frac{1}{|\psi_{ij,h}|}\sum_{t\in\psi_{ij,h}}E_{t,ih}E_{t,jh}\bigg\|^{2}\\
&=O\bigg(\frac{d_{\text{-}k}}{T}+d_{\text{-}k}^{2}d_{k}^{-\alpha_{k,r_{k}}}\bigg).\end{split}

Now consider (
6.42
). Similar to (
6.39
), we have

\begin{split}&\hskip 14.22636pt\bigg\|\sum_{i=1}^{d_{k}}(\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{i\cdot}-\mathbf{H}_{j}\mathbf{Q}_{i\cdot})\sum_{h=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}\frac{1}{|\psi_{ij,h}|}\sum_{t\in\psi_{ij,h}}E_{t,jh}(\mathbf{A}_{\text{-}k})_{h\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{i\cdot}\bigg\|^{2}\\
&\leq\bigg(\sum_{i=1}^{d_{k}}\|\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{i\cdot}-\mathbf{H}_{j}\mathbf{Q}_{i\cdot}\|_{F}^{2}\bigg)\cdot\sum_{i=1}^{d_{k}}\bigg(\sum_{h=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}\frac{1}{|\psi_{ij,h}|}\sum_{t\in\psi_{ij,h}}E_{t,jh}(\mathbf{A}_{\text{-}k})_{h\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{i\cdot}\bigg)^{2}\\
&=O(d_{\text{-}k}^{2})\cdot\bigg(\sum_{i=1}^{d_{k}}\|\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{i\cdot}-\mathbf{H}_{j}\mathbf{Q}_{i\cdot}\|_{F}^{2}\bigg)\cdot\sum_{i=1}^{d_{k}}\|\mathbf{u}_{i}\|^{2}\bigg(\sum_{h=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}\sum_{t\in\psi_{ij,h}}\frac{1}{d_{\text{-}k}\cdot|\psi_{ij,h}|}E_{t,jh}\mathbf{v}_{h}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\frac{1}{\|\mathbf{u}_{i}\|}\mathbf{u}_{i}\bigg)^{2}\\
&=O_{P}\Bigg(d_{k}^{3\alpha_{k,1}-2\alpha_{k,r_{k}}}\bigg(\frac{d_{\text{-}k}}{T}\bigg)\bigg(\frac{1}{Td_{\text{-}k}}+\frac{1}{d_{k}}\bigg)\prod_{j=1}^{K}d_{j}^{2(1-\alpha_{j,1})}\Bigg),\end{split}

where we used Lemma
2
, Proposition
2
.2 and (L1) in the last equality.

Finally, we need the rate of (
6.38
) to be truly dominating.
Using Assumption (AD1), we consider the following ratios with
d_{1},\dots,d_{K},T\to\infty
:

\begin{split}&\hskip 14.22636pt\bigg\|\sum_{i=1}^{d_{k}}(\mathbf{H}_{j}-\mathbf{H}^{a})\mathbf{Q}_{i\cdot}\sum_{h=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}\frac{1}{|\psi_{ij,h}|}\sum_{t\in\psi_{ij,h}}E_{t,jh}(\mathbf{A}_{\text{-}k})_{h\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{i\cdot}\bigg\|^{2}\bigg/\bigg\|\sum_{i=1}^{d_{k}}\mathbf{H}^{a}\mathbf{Q}_{i\cdot}\sum_{h=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}\frac{1}{|\psi_{ij,h}|}\sum_{t\in\psi_{ij,h}}E_{t,jh}(\mathbf{A}_{\text{-}k})_{h\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{i\cdot}\bigg\|^{2}\\
&=O_{P}\Bigg(d_{k}^{2(\alpha_{k,1}-\alpha_{k,r_{k}})}\cdot\frac{1}{T}\Bigg)=o_{P}(1),\\
&\hskip 14.22636pt\bigg\|\sum_{i=1}^{d_{k}}\mathbf{H}_{j}\mathbf{Q}_{i\cdot}\sum_{h=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}\frac{1}{|\psi_{ij,h}|}\sum_{t\in\psi_{ij,h}}E_{t,ih}(\mathbf{A}_{\text{-}k})_{h\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{j\cdot}\bigg\|^{2}\bigg/\bigg\|\sum_{i=1}^{d_{k}}\mathbf{H}^{a}\mathbf{Q}_{i\cdot}\sum_{h=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}\frac{1}{|\psi_{ij,h}|}\sum_{t\in\psi_{ij,h}}E_{t,jh}(\mathbf{A}_{\text{-}k})_{h\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{i\cdot}\bigg\|^{2}\\
&=O_{P}\Big(d_{k}^{-\alpha_{k,1}}\prod_{j\in[K]\setminus\{k\}}d_{j}^{\alpha_{k,j}-1}\Big)=o_{P}(1),\\
&\hskip 14.22636pt\bigg\|\sum_{i=1}^{d_{k}}\mathbf{H}_{j}\mathbf{Q}_{i\cdot}\sum_{h=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}\frac{1}{|\psi_{ij,h}|}\sum_{t\in\psi_{ij,h}}E_{t,ih}E_{t,jh}\bigg\|^{2}\bigg/\bigg\|\sum_{i=1}^{d_{k}}\mathbf{H}^{a}\mathbf{Q}_{i\cdot}\sum_{h=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}\frac{1}{|\psi_{ij,h}|}\sum_{t\in\psi_{ij,h}}E_{t,jh}(\mathbf{A}_{\text{-}k})_{h\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{i\cdot}\bigg\|^{2}\\
&=O_{P}\Big(Td_{\text{-}k}d_{k}^{-\alpha_{k,r_{k}}-\alpha_{k,1}}\Big)=o_{P}(1),\\
&\hskip 14.22636pt\bigg\|\sum_{i=1}^{d_{k}}(\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{i\cdot}-\mathbf{H}_{j}\mathbf{Q}_{i\cdot})\sum_{h=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}\frac{1}{|\psi_{ij,h}|}\sum_{t\in\psi_{ij,h}}E_{t,jh}(\mathbf{A}_{\text{-}k})_{h\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{i\cdot}\bigg\|^{2}\bigg/\bigg\|\sum_{i=1}^{d_{k}}\mathbf{H}^{a}\mathbf{Q}_{i\cdot}\sum_{h=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}\frac{1}{|\psi_{ij,h}|}\sum_{t\in\psi_{ij,h}}E_{t,jh}(\mathbf{A}_{\text{-}k})_{h\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{i\cdot}\bigg\|^{2}\\
&=O_{P}\Bigg(d_{k}^{2(\alpha_{k,1}-\alpha_{k,r_{k}})}\bigg(\frac{1}{Td_{\text{-}k}}+\frac{1}{d_{k}}\bigg)\prod_{j=1}^{K}d_{j}^{2(1-\alpha_{j,1})}\Bigg)=o_{P}(1),\\
&\hskip 14.22636pt\bigg\|\widehat{\mathbf{D}}(\mathbf{H}_{j}-\mathbf{H}^{a})\mathbf{Q}_{j\cdot}\bigg\|^{2}\bigg/\bigg\|\sum_{i=1}^{d_{k}}\mathbf{H}^{a}\mathbf{Q}_{i\cdot}\sum_{h=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}\frac{1}{|\psi_{ij,h}|}\sum_{t\in\psi_{ij,h}}E_{t,jh}(\mathbf{A}_{\text{-}k})_{h\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{i\cdot}\bigg\|^{2},\end{split}

by Assumptions (R1) and the rate assumptions
d_{k}^{2\alpha_{k,1}-3\alpha_{k,r_{k}}}=o(d_{\text{-}k})
and
Td_{\text{-}k}=o(d_{k}^{\alpha_{k,1}+\alpha_{k,r_{k}}})
.
Hence
\mathcal{I}_{H,j}
is indeed the dominating term in (
6.37
). In other words, we have

\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{j\cdot}-\mathbf{H}_{j}\mathbf{Q}_{j\cdot}=\mathcal{I}_{H,j}+o_{P}(1).

(6.47)

Then we want to show that

\begin{split}\sqrt{T\omega_{B}}\cdot\mathcal{I}_{H,j}&=\sqrt{T\omega_{B}}\cdot\widehat{\mathbf{D}}^{-1}\mathbf{H}^{a}\sum_{i=1}^{d_{k}}\mathbf{Q}_{i\cdot}\sum_{h=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}\frac{1}{|\psi_{ij,h}|}\sum_{t\in\psi_{ij,h}}E_{t,jh}(\mathbf{A}_{\text{-}k})_{h\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{i\cdot}\\
&\xrightarrow{p}\sqrt{T\omega_{B}}\cdot\mathbf{D}^{-1}\mathbf{H}_{k}^{a,\ast}\sum_{i=1}^{d_{k}}\mathbf{Q}_{i\cdot}\sum_{h=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}\frac{1}{|\psi_{ij,h}|}\sum_{t\in\psi_{ij,h}}E_{t,jh}(\mathbf{A}_{\text{-}k})_{h\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{i\cdot}\\
&\xrightarrow{\mathcal{D}}\mathcal{N}(\mathbf{0},T\omega_{B}\cdot\mathbf{D}^{-1}\mathbf{H}_{k}^{a,\ast}\mathbf{\Xi}_{k,j}(\mathbf{H}_{k}^{a,\ast})^{\prime}\mathbf{D}^{-1}),\end{split}

(6.48)

where
\mathbf{D}
and
\mathbf{H}_{k}^{a,\ast}
are from Lemma
4
.

We will adapt the central limit theorem for
\alpha
-mixing processes (
Fan and Yao, 2003
, Theorem 2.21). Due to the existence of missing data and the general missing patterns that we allow, we construct an auxiliary time series to facilitate the proof. Formally, define
\{\mathbf{B}_{j,t}\}_{t\in[T]}
as

\begin{split}\mathbf{B}_{j,t}&:=\sqrt{\omega_{B}}\cdot\mathbf{D}^{-1}\mathbf{H}_{k}^{a,\ast}\sum_{i=1}^{d_{k}}\sum_{h=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}\mathbf{Q}_{i\cdot}\frac{T}{|\psi_{ij,h}|}\cdot E_{t,jh}(\mathbf{A}_{\text{-}k})_{h\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{i\cdot}\cdot\mathbbm{1}\{t\in\psi_{ij,h}\}.\end{split}

(6.49)

Hence we have the following,

\begin{split}\sqrt{T\omega_{B}}\cdot\mathcal{I}_{H,j}&\xrightarrow{p}\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{B}_{j,t}.\end{split}

It is easy to see that
\mathbbm{E}[\mathbf{B}_{j,t}]=\mathbf{0}
by Assumption (E1), (E2) and (F1). Moreover,
\mathbf{B}_{j,t}
is also
\alpha
-mixing over
t
. To see this, consider

\begin{split}E_{t,jh}(\mathbf{A}_{\text{-}k})_{h\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{i\cdot}&=\Big(\sum_{q\geq 0}a_{e,q}\mathbf{A}_{e,k,i\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{X}_{e,t-q}\mathbf{A}_{e,\text{-}k,h\cdot}+\Sigma_{\epsilon,k,h,ii}^{1/2}\sum_{q\geq 0}a_{\epsilon,q}(\mathbf{X}_{\epsilon,t-q})_{ih}\Big)(\mathbf{A}_{\text{-}k})_{h\cdot}^{\prime}\Big(\sum_{q\geq 0}a_{f,q}\mathbf{X}_{f,t-q}^{\prime}\Big)\mathbf{A}_{i\cdot}.\end{split}

If we define
\mathbf{b}_{e,t}:=\sum_{q\geq 0}a_{e,q}\mathbf{X}_{e,t-q}
,
\mathbf{b}_{\epsilon,ih,t}:=\sum_{q\geq 0}a_{\epsilon,q}(\mathbf{X}_{\epsilon,t-q})_{ih}
and
\mathbf{b}_{f,t}:=\sum_{q\geq 0}a_{f,q}\mathbf{X}_{f,t-q}
which are independent of each other by Assumption (E2), we can then rewrite

\mathbf{B}_{j,t}=h\Big(\mathbf{b}_{e,t},(\mathbf{b}_{\epsilon,ih,t})_{i\in[d_{k}],h\in[d_{\text{-}k}]},\mathbf{b}_{f,t}\Big),

for some function
h
, and hence Theorem 5.2 in
Bradley, 2005
implies the
\alpha
-mixing property. Then similar to
Chen and Fan, 2023
, it is left to show that there exists an
m>2
such that
\mathbbm{E}[\|\mathbf{B}_{j,t}\|^{m}]\leq C
for some constant
C
. With Assumption (E1), (E2) and (F1) and similar to the proof of Proposition
2
, we have

\begin{split}\mathbbm{E}\bigg[\sum_{h=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}\frac{T}{|\psi_{ij,h}|}\cdot E_{t,jh}\mathbf{u}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{v}\cdot\mathbbm{1}\{t\in\psi_{ij,h}\}\bigg]^{2}=O(d_{\text{-}k}),\end{split}

where
\mathbf{u}\in\mathbbm{R}^{r_{k}}
and
\mathbf{v}\in\mathbbm{R}^{r_{\text{-}k}}
are any deterministic vectors of constant order. Hence

\begin{split}&\hskip 14.22636pt\mathbbm{E}[\|\mathbf{B}_{j,t}\|^{m}]\\
&\leq\omega_{B}^{m/2}\cdot\|\mathbf{D}^{-1}\|_{F}^{m}\cdot\|\mathbf{H}_{k}^{a,\ast}\|_{F}^{m}\cdot\bigg(\sum_{i=1}^{d_{k}}\|\mathbf{Q}_{i\cdot}\|^{2}\bigg)^{m/2}\mathbbm{E}\Bigg\{\bigg[\sum_{i=1}^{d_{k}}\bigg(\sum_{h=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}\frac{T}{|\psi_{ij,h}|}\cdot E_{t,jh}(\mathbf{A}_{\text{-}k})_{h\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{i\cdot}\cdot\mathbbm{1}\{t\in\psi_{ij,h}\}\bigg)^{2}\bigg]^{m/2}\Bigg\}\\
&=O\Big((\omega_{B}d_{\text{-}k}d_{k}^{\alpha_{k,1}})^{m/2}\Big)\cdot\|\mathbf{D}^{-1}\|_{F}^{m}=O_{P}\Bigg(\bigg(\omega_{B}d_{\text{-}k}d_{k}^{3\alpha_{k,1}-2\alpha_{k,r_{k}}}\prod_{j=1}^{K}d_{j}^{-2\alpha_{j,1}}\bigg)^{m/2}\Bigg)=O_{P}(1),\end{split}

where we used Lemma
2
and the definition of
\omega_{B}
in the last line. Theorem 2.21 in
Fan and Yao, 2003
then applies. With (
6.47
), (
6.48
) and Lemma
4
, we can directly establish that

\sqrt{T\omega_{B}}\cdot(\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{j\cdot}-\mathbf{H}_{j}\mathbf{Q}_{j\cdot})\xrightarrow{\mathcal{D}}\mathcal{N}(\mathbf{0},T\omega_{B}\cdot\mathbf{D}^{-1}\mathbf{H}_{k}^{a,\ast}\mathbf{\Xi}_{k,j}(\mathbf{H}_{k}^{a,\ast})^{\prime}\mathbf{D}^{-1}).

(6.50)

Consider now the second term in (
6.36
). By Lemma
3
and
4
, we have

\bigg\|(\mathbf{H}_{j}-\mathbf{H}^{a})\mathbf{Q}_{j\cdot}\bigg\|^{2}\leq\|\mathbf{H}_{j}-\mathbf{H}^{a}\|_{F}^{2}\cdot\|\mathbf{A}_{j\cdot}\|^{2}\cdot\|\mathbf{Z}_{k}^{-1/2}\|^{2}=O_{P}\Bigg(\min\bigg(\frac{1}{T},\frac{\eta^{2}}{(1-\eta)^{2}}\bigg)d_{k}^{2\alpha_{k,1}-3\alpha_{k,r_{k}}}\Bigg),

implying

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt\bigg\|(\mathbf{H}_{j}-\mathbf{H}^{a})\mathbf{Q}_{j\cdot}\bigg\|^{2}\bigg/\bigg\|\widehat{\mathbf{D}}^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{d_{k}}\mathbf{H}^{a}\mathbf{Q}_{i\cdot}\sum_{h=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}\frac{1}{|\psi_{ij,h}|}\sum_{t\in\psi_{ij,h}}E_{t,jh}(\mathbf{A}_{\text{-}k})_{h\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{i\cdot}\bigg\|^{2}

\displaystyle=O_{P}\Bigg(\min\bigg(1,\frac{T\eta^{2}}{(1-\eta)^{2}}\bigg)d_{k}^{3(\alpha_{k,1}-\alpha_{k,r_{k}})}\prod_{j\in[K]\setminus\{k\}}d_{j}^{2\alpha_{j,1}-1}\Bigg),

which is unrealistic to be
o_{P}(1)
in the presence of missing data in general. Thus
(\mathbf{H}_{j}-\mathbf{H}^{a})\mathbf{Q}_{j\cdot}
contributes to the asymptotic distribution of
(\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{j\cdot}-\mathbf{H}^{a}\mathbf{Q}_{j\cdot})
. Rewrite

\begin{split}(\mathbf{H}_{j}-\mathbf{H}^{a})\mathbf{Q}_{j\cdot}&=\widehat{\mathbf{D}}^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{d_{k}}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{i\cdot}\sum_{h=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}\Big(\frac{1}{|\psi_{ij,h}|}\sum_{t\in\psi_{ij,h}}\mathbf{Q}_{i\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{Z,t}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{h\cdot}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{h\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{Z,t}^{\prime}-\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{Q}_{i\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{Z,t}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{h\cdot}\mathbf{\Lambda}_{h\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{Z,t}^{\prime}\Big)\mathbf{Q}_{j\cdot}\\
&=\widehat{\mathbf{D}}^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{d_{k}}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{i\cdot}\mathbf{A}_{i\cdot}^{\prime}\sum_{h=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}\Delta_{F,k,ij,h}\mathbf{Z}_{k}^{1/2}\mathbf{Q}_{j\cdot}\\
&=\widehat{\mathbf{D}}^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{d_{k}}(\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{i\cdot}-\mathbf{H}^{a}\mathbf{Q}_{i\cdot})\mathbf{A}_{i\cdot}^{\prime}\sum_{h=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}\Delta_{F,k,ij,h}\mathbf{Z}_{k}^{1/2}\mathbf{Q}_{j\cdot}+\widehat{\mathbf{D}}^{-1}\mathbf{H}^{a}\sum_{i=1}^{d_{k}}\mathbf{Q}_{i\cdot}\mathbf{A}_{i\cdot}^{\prime}\sum_{h=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}\Delta_{F,k,ij,h}\mathbf{Z}_{k}^{1/2}\mathbf{Q}_{j\cdot}.\end{split}

Note the first term is dominated by the second term due to Theorem
1
. Using Assumption (AD2) and the Slutsky’s theorem, we have

\sqrt{Td_{k}^{\alpha_{k,r_{k}}}}\cdot\widehat{\mathbf{D}}^{-1}\mathbf{H}^{a}\sum_{i=1}^{d_{k}}\mathbf{Q}_{i\cdot}\mathbf{A}_{i\cdot}^{\prime}\sum_{h=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}\Delta_{F,k,ij,h}\mathbf{Z}_{k}^{1/2}\mathbf{Q}_{j\cdot}\to\mathcal{N}(\mathbf{0},\mathbf{D}^{-1}\mathbf{H}_{k}^{a,\ast}h_{k,j}(\mathbf{A}_{j\cdot})(\mathbf{H}_{k}^{a,\ast})^{\prime}\mathbf{D}^{-1})\;\;\;\text{$\mathcal{G}^{T}$-stably}.

(6.51)

Furthermore,
\mathcal{I}_{H,j}
and
(\mathbf{H}_{j}-\mathbf{H}^{a})\mathbf{Q}_{j\cdot}
are asymptotically independent since the randomness of
\mathcal{I}_{H,j}
comes from
E_{t,jh}(\mathbf{A}_{\text{-}k})_{h\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}
while that of
(\mathbf{H}_{j}-\mathbf{H}^{a})\mathbf{Q}_{j\cdot}
comes from
\Delta_{F,k,ij,h}
. From (
6.50
) and (
6.51
), we conclude that
