\sqrt{Td_{k}^{\alpha_{k,r_{k}}}}\cdot(\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{j\cdot}-\mathbf{H}^{a}\mathbf{Q}_{j\cdot})\xrightarrow{\mathcal{D}}\mathcal{N}(\mathbf{0},\mathbf{D}^{-1}\mathbf{H}_{k}^{a,\ast}(Td_{k}^{\alpha_{k,r_{k}}}\cdot\mathbf{\Xi}_{k,j}+h_{k,j}(\mathbf{A}_{j\cdot}))(\mathbf{H}_{k}^{a,\ast})^{\prime}\mathbf{D}^{-1}).

On the other hand, if we have finite missingness or asymptotically vanishing missingness such that

\min\bigg(1,\frac{T\eta^{2}}{(1-\eta)^{2}}\bigg)d_{k}^{3(\alpha_{k,1}-\alpha_{k,r_{k}})}\prod_{j\in[K]\setminus\{k\}}d_{j}^{2\alpha_{j,1}-1}=Td^{-1}g_{s}^{2}g_{\eta}d_{k}^{1+\alpha_{k,1}-3\alpha_{k,r_{k}}}=o(1),

then (
6.51
) is dominated by (
6.50
), and hence it holds at the absence of (AD2) that

\sqrt{T\omega_{B}}\cdot(\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{j\cdot}-\mathbf{H}^{a}\mathbf{Q}_{j\cdot})\xrightarrow{\mathcal{D}}\mathcal{N}(\mathbf{0},T\omega_{B}\cdot\mathbf{D}^{-1}\mathbf{H}_{k}^{a,\ast}\mathbf{\Xi}_{k,j}(\mathbf{H}_{k}^{a,\ast})^{\prime}\mathbf{D}^{-1}).

This completes the proof of Theorem
3
.

\square

Proof of Theorem
4
.

By Lemma
4
,
\widehat{\mathbf{D}}_{k}
is consistent for
\mathbf{D}_{k}
, and
\mathbf{H}_{k}^{a}
is consistent for
\mathbf{H}_{k}^{a,\ast}
. Similar to the proof of Theorem 5 in
Chen and Fan, 2023
, it suffice to prove that the HAC estimator
\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{HAC}
based on
\{\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{k},\text{mat}_{k}({\widehat{\mathcal{C}}_{t}}),\text{mat}_{k}({\widehat{\mathcal{E}}_{t}})\}_{t\in[T]}
is a consistent estimator for
\mathbf{H}_{k}^{a}\mathbf{\Xi}_{k,j}(\mathbf{H}_{k}^{a})^{\prime}
. Recall that

\begin{split}\mathbf{H}_{k}^{a}\mathbf{\Xi}_{k,j}(\mathbf{H}_{k}^{a})^{\prime}&=\text{Var}\bigg(\sum_{i=1}^{d_{k}}\mathbf{H}_{k}^{a}\mathbf{Q}_{k,i\cdot}\sum_{h=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}\frac{1}{|\psi_{k,ij,h}|}\sum_{t\in\psi_{k,ij,h}}E_{t,jh}(\mathbf{A}_{\text{-}k})_{h\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{k,i\cdot}\bigg)\\
&=\text{Var}\bigg(\sum_{i=1}^{d_{k}}\Big(\widehat{\mathbf{D}}_{k}^{-1}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{k}^{\prime}\mathbf{Q}_{k}\mathbf{F}_{Z,t}\mathbf{\Lambda}^{\prime}\mathbf{\Lambda}\mathbf{F}_{Z,t}^{\prime}\Big)\mathbf{Q}_{k,i\cdot}\sum_{h=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}\frac{1}{|\psi_{k,ij,h}|}\sum_{t\in\psi_{k,ij,h}}E_{t,jh}(\mathbf{A}_{\text{-}k})_{h\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{k,i\cdot}\bigg)\\
&=\text{Var}\bigg(\sum_{i=1}^{d_{k}}\Big(\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\mathbf{D}}_{k}^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{k}^{\prime}\cdot\text{mat}_{k}({\mathcal{C}_{t}})\cdot\text{mat}_{k}({\mathcal{C}_{t}})_{i\cdot}\Big)\\
&\;\;\;\cdot\sum_{h=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}\frac{1}{|\psi_{k,ij,h}|}\sum_{t\in\psi_{k,ij,h}}\text{mat}_{k}({\mathcal{E}_{t}})_{jh}\cdot\text{mat}_{k}({\mathcal{C}_{t}})_{ih}\bigg).\end{split}

By Theorem
1
, and the rate assumption in the statement of Theorem
4
, we have
\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{k}
being consistent for a version of
\mathbf{Q}_{k}
(in Frobenius norm) for any
k\in[K]
. By Theorem
2
and the assumption that the rate for individual common component imputation error is going to 0,
\widehat{\mathcal{C}}_{t,i_{1},\dots,i_{K}}
is consistent for
\mathcal{C}_{t,i_{1},\dots,i_{K}}
for any
k\in[K],i_{k}\in[d_{k}],t\in[T]
. Hence, it also holds that
\widehat{\mathcal{E}}_{t,i_{1},\dots,i_{K}}
is consistent for
\mathcal{E}_{t,i_{1},\dots,i_{K}}
for any
k\in[K],i_{k}\in[d_{k}],t\in[T]
. We can finally conclude that
\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{HAC}
is estimating
\mathbf{H}_{k}^{a}\mathbf{\Xi}_{k,j}(\mathbf{H}_{k}^{a})^{\prime}
consistently
(
Newey and West, 1987
)
, which is result 1. We can also show a similar result for
\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{HAC}^{\Delta}
, which is result 2 (details omitted). Combining both results, and consider the general statement of Theorem
3
, we can easily conclude result 3. This completes the proof of the theorem.

\square

We will present two other lemmas before proving Theorem
2
. While we stick with the notations in (
6.2
), we use the following also hereafter:

\begin{split}\mathbf{y}_{t}&:=\textbf{vec}\big(\mathcal{Y}_{t}\big),\;\mathbf{m}_{t}:=\textbf{vec}\big(\mathcal{M}_{t}\big),\;\mathbf{f}_{Z,t}:=\textbf{vec}\big(\mathcal{F}_{Z,t}\big),\;\bm{\varepsilon}_{t}:=\textbf{vec}\big(\mathcal{E}_{t}\big),\mathbf{c}_{t}:=\textbf{vec}\big(\mathcal{C}_{t}\big),\;\mathbf{f}_{t}:=\textbf{vec}\big(\mathcal{F}_{t}\big),\\
\mathbf{H}_{\otimes}&:=\mathbf{H}_{K}^{a}\otimes\dots\otimes\mathbf{H}_{1}^{a},\;\mathbf{A}_{\otimes}:=\mathbf{A}_{K}\otimes\dots\otimes\mathbf{A}_{1},\;\mathbf{Z}_{\otimes}:=\mathbf{Z}_{K}\otimes\dots\otimes\mathbf{Z}_{1},\end{split}

(6.52)

where the hat versions (if any) of the above are defined similarly.

Lemma 5

Under the assumptions in Theorem
2
, for any
k\in[K]
and
j\in[d_{k}]
,

\displaystyle\|\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{k,j\cdot}-\mathbf{H}_{k}^{a}\mathbf{Q}_{k,j\cdot}\|_{F}^{2}

\displaystyle=O_{P}\Big(T^{-1}d_{\text{-}k}d_{k}^{3\alpha_{k,1}-2\alpha_{k,r_{k}}}g_{s}^{-2}+d^{2}g_{s}^{-2}d_{k}^{2\alpha_{k,1}-3\alpha_{k,r_{k}}-2}+g_{\eta}d_{k}^{2\alpha_{k,1}-3\alpha_{k,r_{k}}}\Big).

(6.53)

Proof of Lemma
5
.

First, consider the case when
Td_{\text{-}k}=o\big(d_{k}^{\alpha_{k,r_{k}}+\alpha_{k,1}}\big)
. From (
6.50
) in the proof of Theorem 3, we have
\|\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{k,j\cdot}-\mathbf{H}_{k,j}\mathbf{Q}_{k,j\cdot}\|_{F}^{2}=O_{P}(T^{-1}\omega_{B}^{-1})
. Hence it follows that

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt\|\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{k,j\cdot}-\mathbf{H}_{k}^{a}\mathbf{Q}_{k,j\cdot}\|_{F}^{2}=O_{P}\Big(\|\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{k,j\cdot}-\mathbf{H}_{k,j}\mathbf{Q}_{k,j\cdot}\|_{F}^{2}+\|(\mathbf{H}_{k,j}-\mathbf{H}_{k}^{a})\mathbf{Q}_{k,j\cdot}\|_{F}^{2}\Big)

\displaystyle=O_{P}\Big((T\omega_{B})^{-1}+g_{\eta}d_{k}^{2\alpha_{k,1}-3\alpha_{k,r_{k}}}\Big)=O_{P}\Big(T^{-1}d_{\text{-}k}d_{k}^{3\alpha_{k,1}-2\alpha_{k,r_{k}}}g_{s}^{-2}+g_{\eta}d_{k}^{2\alpha_{k,1}-3\alpha_{k,r_{k}}}\Big),

(6.54)

where we used Lemma
3
in the second equality, and

\displaystyle\big\|\mathbf{Q}_{k,j\cdot}\big\|^{2}

\displaystyle=\|\mathbf{Z}_{k}^{-1/2}\mathbf{A}_{k,j\cdot}\|^{2}=O_{P}(d_{k}^{-\alpha_{k,r_{k}}}).

Now suppose
Td_{\text{-}k}=o\big(d_{k}^{\alpha_{k,r_{k}}+\alpha_{k,1}}\big)
fails to hold. From the decomposition of
\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{k,j\cdot}-\mathbf{H}_{k,j}\mathbf{Q}_{k,j\cdot}
in (
6.37
), the leading term among the expressions from (
6.38
) to (
6.44
) will be (
6.41
). It has rate

O_{P}\bigg(\frac{d_{\text{-}k}}{T}+d_{\text{-}k}^{2}d_{k}^{-\alpha_{k,r_{k}}}\bigg)=O_{P}(d_{\text{-}k}^{2}d_{k}^{-\alpha_{k,r_{k}}}),

where the above equality used the fact that
Td_{\text{-}k}=o\big(d_{k}^{\alpha_{k,r_{k}}+\alpha_{k,1}}\big)
does not hold. Together with the bound on
\|\widehat{\mathbf{D}}_{k}^{-1}\|_{F}
from Lemma
2
, we have

\|\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{k,j\cdot}-\mathbf{H}_{k,j}\mathbf{Q}_{k,j\cdot}\|_{F}^{2}=O_{P}\Big(d^{2}g_{s}^{-2}d_{k}^{2\alpha_{k,1}-3\alpha_{k,r_{k}}-2}\Big).

(6.55)

Combining (
6.54
) and (
6.55
), we arrive at the statement of the lemma.

\square

Lemma 6

Under the Assumptions in Theorem
2
, with the notations in (
6.2
) and (
6.52
), we have the following for any
j\in[d]
:

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{Q}_{\otimes}\mathbf{H}_{\otimes}^{\prime}\|_{F}^{2}=O_{P}(1),

(6.56)

\displaystyle\|\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{\otimes,j\cdot}-\mathbf{H}_{\otimes}\mathbf{Q}_{\otimes,j\cdot}\|^{2}

\displaystyle=O_{P}\Bigg(\max_{k\in[K]}\Bigg\{T^{-1}d_{\text{-}k}d_{k}^{3\alpha_{k,1}-\alpha_{k,r_{k}}}g_{s}^{-2}g_{w}^{-1}+d^{2}g_{s}^{-2}g_{w}^{-1}d_{k}^{2\alpha_{k,1}-2\alpha_{k,r_{k}}-2}+g_{\eta}g_{w}^{-1}d_{k}^{2\alpha_{k,1}-2\alpha_{k,r_{k}}}\Bigg\}\Bigg),

(6.57)

\displaystyle\|\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{\otimes}-\mathbf{Q}_{\otimes}\mathbf{H}_{\otimes}^{\prime}\|_{F}^{2}

\displaystyle=O_{P}\Bigg(\max_{k\in[K]}\Bigg\{T^{-1}dd_{k}^{3\alpha_{k,1}-2\alpha_{k,r_{k}}}g_{s}^{-2}+d^{2}g_{s}^{-2}d_{k}^{2\alpha_{k,1}-3\alpha_{k,r_{k}}-1}+g_{\eta}d_{k}^{2\alpha_{k,1}-3\alpha_{k,r_{k}}+1}\Bigg\}\Bigg).

(6.58)

Proof of Lemma
6
.

For (
6.56
), we have

\begin{split}\|\mathbf{Q}_{\otimes}\mathbf{H}_{\otimes}^{\prime}\|_{F}^{2}&\leq\big\|\mathbf{H}_{\otimes}\big\|_{F}^{2}\cdot\prod_{k=1}^{K}\big\|\mathbf{Q}_{k}\big\|_{F}^{2}=O_{P}(1),\end{split}

where we used Assumption (L1) in the last equality.

To show (
6.57
), for any
j\in[d_{k}]
, by a simple induction argument (omitted),

\begin{split}&\|\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{\otimes,j\cdot}-\mathbf{H}_{\otimes}\mathbf{Q}_{\otimes,j\cdot}\|^{2}=\|(\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{\otimes}-\mathbf{Q}_{\otimes}\mathbf{H}_{\otimes}^{\prime})_{j\cdot}\|^{2}=\Big\|[(\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{K}\otimes\dots\otimes\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{1})-(\mathbf{Q}_{K}\mathbf{H}_{K}^{\prime}\otimes\dots\otimes\mathbf{Q}_{1}\mathbf{H}_{1}^{\prime})]_{j\cdot}\Big\|^{2}\\
&\leq\sum_{k=1}^{K}\bigg\{\max_{j\in[d_{k}]}\big\|\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{k,j\cdot}-\mathbf{H}_{k}^{a}\mathbf{Q}_{k,j\cdot}\big\|^{2}\prod_{\ell\in[K]\setminus\{k\}}\max_{j\in[d_{\ell}]}\big\|\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{\ell,j\cdot}\big\|^{2}\bigg\}\\
&=O_{P}\Bigg(\max_{k\in[K]}\Bigg\{\Bigg[T^{-1}d_{\text{-}k}d_{k}^{3\alpha_{k,1}-2\alpha_{k,r_{k}}}g_{s}^{-2}+d^{2}g_{s}^{-2}d_{k}^{2\alpha_{k,1}-3\alpha_{k,r_{k}}-2}+g_{\eta}d_{k}^{2\alpha_{k,1}-3\alpha_{k,r_{k}}}\Bigg]\cdot\prod_{\ell\in[K]\setminus\{k\}}d_{\ell}^{-\alpha_{\ell,r_{\ell}}}\Bigg\}\Bigg)\\
&=O_{P}\Bigg(\max_{k\in[K]}\Bigg\{T^{-1}d_{\text{-}k}d_{k}^{3\alpha_{k,1}-\alpha_{k,r_{k}}}g_{s}^{-2}g_{w}^{-1}+d^{2}g_{s}^{-2}g_{w}^{-1}d_{k}^{2\alpha_{k,1}-2\alpha_{k,r_{k}}-2}+g_{\eta}g_{w}^{-1}d_{k}^{2\alpha_{k,1}-2\alpha_{k,r_{k}}}\Bigg\}\Bigg),\end{split}

where the second last equality used (
6.53
) and

\displaystyle\big\|\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{\ell,j\cdot}\big\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2(\big\|\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{\ell,j\cdot}-\mathbf{H}_{k}^{a}\mathbf{Q}_{\ell,j\cdot}\big\|^{2}+\big\|\mathbf{H}_{\ell}^{a}\mathbf{Q}_{\ell,j\cdot}\big\|^{2})=O_{P}(\big\|\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{\ell,j\cdot}-\mathbf{H}_{\ell}^{a}\mathbf{Q}_{\ell,j\cdot}\big\|^{2}+\big\|\mathbf{H}_{\ell}^{a}\mathbf{Z}_{\ell}^{-1/2}\mathbf{A}_{\ell,j\cdot}\big\|^{2})

\displaystyle=O_{P}(\big\|\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{\ell,j\cdot}-\mathbf{H}_{\ell}^{a}\mathbf{Q}_{\ell,j\cdot}\big\|^{2}+\big\|\mathbf{H}_{\ell}^{a}\big\|_{F}^{2}\cdot(d_{\ell}^{-\alpha_{\ell,r_{\ell}}/2})^{2}\cdot 1)=O_{P}(d_{\ell}^{-\alpha_{\ell,r_{\ell}}}).

Finally it also holds that

\displaystyle\|\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{\otimes}-

\displaystyle\mathbf{Q}_{\otimes}\mathbf{H}_{\otimes}^{\prime}\|_{F}^{2}=\|(\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{K}\otimes\dots\otimes\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{1})-(\mathbf{Q}_{K}\mathbf{H}_{K}^{a^{\prime}}\otimes\dots\otimes\mathbf{Q}_{1}\mathbf{H}_{1}^{a^{\prime}})\|_{F}^{2}=O(1)\cdot\sum_{k=1}^{K}\|\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{k}-\mathbf{Q}_{k}\mathbf{H}_{k}^{a^{\prime}}\|_{F}^{2}

\displaystyle=O\Bigg(\max_{k\in[K]}\sum_{j=1}^{d_{k}}\|\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{k,j\cdot}-\mathbf{H}_{k}^{a}\mathbf{Q}_{k,j\cdot}\|^{2}\Bigg)

\displaystyle=O_{P}\Bigg(\max_{k\in[K]}\Bigg\{T^{-1}dd_{k}^{3\alpha_{k,1}-2\alpha_{k,r_{k}}}g_{s}^{-2}+d^{2}g_{s}^{-2}d_{k}^{2\alpha_{k,1}-3\alpha_{k,r_{k}}-1}+g_{\eta}d_{k}^{2\alpha_{k,1}-3\alpha_{k,r_{k}}+1}\Bigg\}\Bigg),

where the second equality could be shown by a simple induction argument using
\big\|\mathbf{Q}_{k}\big\|=O(1)
(omitted), and the last equality is from (
6.53
).

\square

Proof of Theorem
2
.

The equation (
3.6
) is essentially

\displaystyle\widehat{\mathbf{f}}_{Z,t}

\displaystyle=\bigg(\sum_{j=1}^{d}m_{t,j}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{\otimes,j\cdot}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{\otimes,j\cdot}^{\prime}\bigg)^{-1}\bigg(\sum_{j=1}^{d}m_{t,j}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{\otimes,j\cdot}y_{t,j}\bigg)

\displaystyle=\bigg(\sum_{j=1}^{d}m_{t,j}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{\otimes,j\cdot}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{\otimes,j\cdot}^{\prime}\bigg)^{-1}\bigg(\sum_{j=1}^{d}m_{t,j}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{\otimes,j\cdot}(\mathbf{Q}_{\otimes,j\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{f}_{Z,t}+\varepsilon_{t,j})\bigg)

\displaystyle=\bigg(\sum_{j=1}^{d}m_{t,j}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{\otimes,j\cdot}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{\otimes,j\cdot}^{\prime}\bigg)^{-1}\bigg(\sum_{j=1}^{d}m_{t,j}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{\otimes,j\cdot}\mathbf{Q}_{\otimes,j\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{f}_{Z,t}\bigg)+\bigg(\sum_{j=1}^{d}m_{t,j}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{\otimes,j\cdot}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{\otimes,j\cdot}^{\prime}\bigg)^{-1}\bigg(\sum_{j=1}^{d}m_{t,j}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{\otimes,j\cdot}\varepsilon_{t,j}\bigg)

\displaystyle=\bigg(\sum_{j=1}^{d}m_{t,j}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{\otimes,j\cdot}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{\otimes,j\cdot}^{\prime}\bigg)^{-1}\bigg(\sum_{j=1}^{d}m_{t,j}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{\otimes,j\cdot}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{\otimes,j\cdot}^{\prime}\bigg)(\mathbf{H}_{\otimes}^{\prime})^{-1}\mathbf{f}_{Z,t}

\displaystyle\;\;\;+\bigg(\sum_{j=1}^{d}m_{t,j}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{\otimes,j\cdot}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{\otimes,j\cdot}^{\prime}\bigg)^{-1}\bigg(\sum_{j=1}^{d}m_{t,j}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{\otimes,j\cdot}\varepsilon_{t,j}\bigg)

\displaystyle\;\;\;+\bigg(\sum_{j=1}^{d}m_{t,j}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{\otimes,j\cdot}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{\otimes,j\cdot}^{\prime}\bigg)^{-1}\bigg(\sum_{j=1}^{d}m_{t,j}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{\otimes,j\cdot}(\mathbf{H}_{\otimes}\mathbf{Q}_{\otimes,j\cdot}-\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{\otimes,j\cdot})^{\prime}\bigg)(\mathbf{H}_{\otimes}^{\prime})^{-1}\mathbf{f}_{Z,t}

\displaystyle=:(\mathbf{H}_{\otimes}^{\prime})^{-1}\mathbf{f}_{Z,t}+\widetilde{\bm{\varepsilon}}_{H,t}+\widetilde{\bm{\varepsilon}}_{t}+\widetilde{\mathbf{f}}_{Z,t},\;\text{ where }

(6.59)

\displaystyle\widetilde{\bm{\varepsilon}}_{H,t}

\displaystyle:=\bigg(\sum_{j=1}^{d}m_{t,j}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{\otimes,j\cdot}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{\otimes,j\cdot}^{\prime}\bigg)^{-1}\bigg(\sum_{j=1}^{d}m_{t,j}\mathbf{H}_{\otimes}\mathbf{Q}_{\otimes,j\cdot}\varepsilon_{t,j}\bigg),

\displaystyle\widetilde{\bm{\varepsilon}}_{t}

\displaystyle:=\bigg(\sum_{j=1}^{d}m_{t,j}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{\otimes,j\cdot}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{\otimes,j\cdot}^{\prime}\bigg)^{-1}\bigg(\sum_{j=1}^{d}m_{t,j}(\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{\otimes,j\cdot}-\mathbf{H}_{\otimes}\mathbf{Q}_{\otimes,j\cdot})\varepsilon_{t,j}\bigg),

\displaystyle\widetilde{\mathbf{f}}_{Z,t}

\displaystyle:=\bigg(\sum_{j=1}^{d}m_{t,j}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{\otimes,j\cdot}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{\otimes,j\cdot}^{\prime}\bigg)^{-1}\bigg(\sum_{j=1}^{d}m_{t,j}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{\otimes,j\cdot}(\mathbf{H}_{\otimes}\mathbf{Q}_{\otimes,j\cdot}-\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{\otimes,j\cdot})^{\prime}\bigg)(\mathbf{H}_{\otimes}^{\prime})^{-1}\mathbf{f}_{Z,t}.

Then we have

\begin{split}&\hskip 14.22636pt\bigg\|\sum_{j=1}^{d}m_{t,j}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{\otimes,j\cdot}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{\otimes,j\cdot}^{\prime}-\sum_{j=1}^{d}m_{t,j}\mathbf{H}_{\otimes}\mathbf{Q}_{\otimes,j\cdot}\mathbf{Q}_{\otimes,j\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{H}_{\otimes}^{\prime}\bigg\|_{F}\\
&\leq\sum_{j=1}^{d}\bigg\|\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{\otimes,j\cdot}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{\otimes,j\cdot}^{\prime}-\mathbf{H}_{\otimes}\mathbf{Q}_{\otimes,j\cdot}\mathbf{Q}_{\otimes,j\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{H}_{\otimes}^{\prime}\bigg\|_{F}\\
&\leq\sum_{j=1}^{d}\|\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{\otimes,j\cdot}-\mathbf{H}_{\otimes}\mathbf{Q}_{\otimes,j\cdot}\|^{2}+2\sum_{j=1}^{d}\|\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{\otimes,j\cdot}-\mathbf{H}_{\otimes}\mathbf{Q}_{\otimes,j\cdot}\|\cdot\|\mathbf{H}_{\otimes}\mathbf{Q}_{\otimes,j\cdot}\|\\
&=O_{P}(\big\|\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{\otimes}-\mathbf{Q}_{\otimes}\mathbf{H}_{\otimes}^{\prime}\big\|_{F}^{2}+\big\|\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{\otimes}-\mathbf{Q}_{\otimes}\mathbf{H}_{\otimes}^{\prime}\big\|_{F})\\
&=O_{P}\Bigg(\max_{k\in[K]}\Bigg\{T^{-1}dd_{k}^{3\alpha_{k,1}-2\alpha_{k,r_{k}}}g_{s}^{-2}+d^{2}g_{s}^{-2}d_{k}^{2\alpha_{k,1}-3\alpha_{k,r_{k}}-1}+g_{\eta}d_{k}^{2\alpha_{k,1}-3\alpha_{k,r_{k}}+1}\Bigg\}^{1/2}\Bigg)=o_{P}(1),\end{split}

where we used the Cauchy–Schwarz inequality, (
6.56
) and (
6.58
) in the last equality, and Assumption (R1). Hence we have

\begin{split}\sum_{j=1}^{d}m_{t,j}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{\otimes,j\cdot}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{\otimes,j\cdot}^{\prime}&\xrightarrow{p}\sum_{j=1}^{d}m_{t,j}\mathbf{H}_{\otimes}\mathbf{Q}_{\otimes,j\cdot}\mathbf{Q}_{\otimes,j\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{H}_{\otimes}^{\prime}.\end{split}

(6.60)

Note that by (
6.35
) we have
\|\mathbf{H}_{\otimes}^{-1}\|_{F}=O_{P}(1)\cdot\|\mathbf{Q}_{K}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{K}\otimes\dots\otimes\mathbf{Q}_{1}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{1}\|=O_{P}(1)
, which will be used later in the proof. To bound
\mathbf{f}_{Z,t}
, from Assumption (F1), we have

\begin{split}\mathbbm{E}\|\mathbf{f}_{t}\|^{2}&=r=O(1),\end{split}

and hence with Assumption (L1),

\begin{split}\|\mathbf{f}_{Z,t}\|^{2}&\leq\|\mathbf{Z}_{\otimes}^{1/2}\|_{F}^{2}\cdot\|\mathbf{f}_{t}\|^{2}=O_{P}\Bigg(\prod_{j=1}^{K}d_{j}^{\alpha_{j,1}}\Bigg)=O_{P}(g_{s}).\end{split}

(6.61)

With (
6.58
), (
6.60
) and the above result,

\begin{split}\|\widetilde{\mathbf{f}}_{Z,t}\|^{2}&=O_{P}(1)\cdot\bigg(\sum_{j=1}^{d}\|\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{\otimes,j\cdot}\|^{2}\bigg)\bigg(\sum_{j=1}^{d}\|\mathbf{H}_{\otimes}\mathbf{Q}_{\otimes,j\cdot}-\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{\otimes,j\cdot}\|^{2}\bigg)\\
&\cdot\|(\bm{\Sigma}_{A,K}\otimes\dots\otimes\bm{\Sigma}_{A,1})^{-1}\|_{F}^{2}\cdot\|\mathbf{H}_{\otimes}^{-1}\|_{F}^{6}\cdot\|\mathbf{f}_{Z,t}\|^{2}\\
&=O_{P}\Bigg(\max_{k\in[K]}\Bigg\{T^{-1}dd_{k}^{3\alpha_{k,1}-2\alpha_{k,r_{k}}}g_{s}^{-1}+d^{2}g_{s}^{-1}d_{k}^{2\alpha_{k,1}-3\alpha_{k,r_{k}}-1}+g_{\eta}g_{s}d_{k}^{2\alpha_{k,1}-3\alpha_{k,r_{k}}+1}\Bigg\}\Bigg),\end{split}

(6.62)

where we also used Assumption (L1) in the last equality. Similarly, by (
6.57
),

\begin{split}\|\widetilde{\bm{\varepsilon}}_{t}\|^{2}&=O_{P}(1)\cdot\|(\bm{\Sigma}_{A,K}\otimes\dots\otimes\bm{\Sigma}_{A,1})^{-1}\|_{F}^{2}\cdot\|\mathbf{H}_{\otimes}^{-1}\|_{F}^{4}\cdot O_{P}\big(\max_{j\in[d]}\|\mathbf{H}_{\otimes}\mathbf{Q}_{\otimes,j\cdot}-\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{\otimes,j\cdot}\|^{2}\big)\bigg(\sum_{j,\ell=1}^{d}|\mathbbm{E}\varepsilon_{t,j}\varepsilon_{t,\ell}|\bigg)\\
&=O_{P}\Bigg(\max_{k\in[K]}\Bigg\{T^{-1}dd_{\text{-}k}d_{k}^{3\alpha_{k,1}-\alpha_{k,r_{k}}}g_{s}^{-2}g_{w}^{-1}+d^{3}g_{s}^{-2}g_{w}^{-1}d_{k}^{2\alpha_{k,1}-2\alpha_{k,r_{k}}-2}+dg_{\eta}g_{w}^{-1}d_{k}^{2\alpha_{k,1}-2\alpha_{k,r_{k}}}\Bigg\}\Bigg),\end{split}

(6.63)

since
\sum_{j,\ell=1}^{d}|\mathbbm{E}\varepsilon_{t,j}\varepsilon_{t,\ell}|=O(d)
by Assumption (E1). By the same token,

\begin{split}\|\widetilde{\bm{\varepsilon}}_{H,t}\|^{2}&=O_{P}(1)\cdot\|(\bm{\Sigma}_{A,K}\otimes\dots\otimes\bm{\Sigma}_{A,1})^{-1}\|_{F}^{2}\cdot\|\mathbf{H}_{\otimes}^{-1}\|_{F}^{2}\cdot\|\mathbf{Z}_{\otimes}^{-1/2}\|_{F}^{2}\cdot\bigg\|\sum_{j=1}^{d}m_{t,j}\mathbf{A}_{\otimes,j\cdot}\varepsilon_{t,j}\bigg\|^{2}=O_{P}(d/g_{w}),\end{split}

(6.64)

where we used

\displaystyle\mathbbm{E}\bigg\|\sum_{j=1}^{d}m_{t,j}\mathbf{A}_{\otimes,j\cdot}\epsilon_{t,j}\bigg\|^{2}\leq\max_{j\in[d]}\big\|\mathbf{A}_{\otimes,j\cdot}\big\|_{F}^{2}\sum_{j,\ell=1}^{d}|\mathbbm{E}\epsilon_{t,j}\epsilon_{t,\ell}|=O(d).

Therefore, we have from (
6.62
), (
6.64
) and (
6.63
),

\displaystyle\|\widehat{\mathbf{f}}_{Z,t}-(\mathbf{H}_{\otimes}^{\prime})^{-1}\mathbf{f}_{Z,t}\|^{2}\leq\|\widetilde{\bm{\varepsilon}}_{H,t}\|^{2}+\|\widetilde{\bm{\varepsilon}}_{t}\|^{2}+\|\widetilde{\mathbf{f}}_{Z,t}\|^{2}

\displaystyle=O_{P}\Bigg(\max_{k\in[K]}\Bigg\{T^{-1}dd_{k}^{3\alpha_{k,1}-2\alpha_{k,r_{k}}}g_{s}^{-1}+d^{2}g_{s}^{-1}d_{k}^{2\alpha_{k,1}-3\alpha_{k,r_{k}}-1}+g_{\eta}g_{s}d_{k}^{2\alpha_{k,1}-3\alpha_{k,r_{k}}+1}\Bigg\}+\frac{d}{g_{w}}\Bigg),

where we also used
1/2<\alpha_{k,r_{k}}\leq\alpha_{k,1}\leq 1
from Assumption (L1) to conclude that
dg_{s}^{-1}g_{w}^{-1}=o(1)
, so that in fact
\|\widetilde{\bm{\varepsilon}}_{t}\|^{2}=o_{P}(\|\widetilde{\mathbf{f}}_{Z,t}\|^{2})
.

Now from (
6.59
) and using the notations in (
6.52
), we can obtain the vectorized imputed values, which are the vectorized estimated common components, as
\widehat{\mathbf{c}}_{t}=\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{\otimes}\widehat{\mathbf{f}}_{Z,t}
for any
t\in[T]
. Then for
j\in[d]
, we have the squared individual imputation error as

\begin{split}&(\widehat{\mathcal{C}}_{t,i_{1},\dots,i_{K}}-\mathcal{C}_{t,i_{1},\dots,i_{K}})^{2}=(\widehat{\mathbf{c}}_{t}-\mathbf{c}_{t})_{j}^{2}=(\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{\otimes,j\cdot}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{f}}_{Z,t}-\mathbf{Q}_{\otimes,j\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{f}_{Z,t})^{2}\\
&=\bigg[\bigg(\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{\otimes,j\cdot}-\mathbf{H}_{\otimes}\mathbf{Q}_{\otimes,j\cdot}\bigg)^{\prime}\bigg((\mathbf{H}_{\otimes}^{\prime})^{-1}\mathbf{f}_{Z,t}+\widetilde{\bm{\varepsilon}}_{H,t}+\widetilde{\bm{\varepsilon}}_{t}+\widetilde{\mathbf{f}}_{Z,t}\bigg)+\mathbf{A}_{\otimes,j\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{Z}_{\otimes}^{-1/2}\mathbf{H}_{\otimes}^{\prime}\bigg(\widetilde{\bm{\varepsilon}}_{H,t}+\widetilde{\bm{\varepsilon}}_{t}+\widetilde{\mathbf{f}}_{Z,t}\bigg)\bigg]^{2}\\
&=O_{P}\Bigg(\max_{k\in[K]}\Bigg\{T^{-1}dd_{k}^{3\alpha_{k,1}-2\alpha_{k,r_{k}}}g_{s}^{-1}g_{w}^{-1}+d^{2}g_{s}^{-1}g_{w}^{-1}d_{k}^{2\alpha_{k,1}-3\alpha_{k,r_{k}}-1}+g_{\eta}g_{s}g_{w}^{-1}d_{k}^{2\alpha_{k,1}-3\alpha_{k,r_{k}}+1}\Bigg\}+\frac{d}{g_{w}^{2}}\Bigg),\end{split}

where we used (
6.57
), (
6.58
) and Assumption (R1) in the last equality.

Lastly, we have the average imputation error as the following,

\begin{split}&\hskip 14.22636pt\frac{1}{Td}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{i_{1},\dots,i_{K}=1}^{d_{1},\dots,d_{K}}(\widehat{\mathcal{C}}_{t,i_{1},\dots,i_{K}}-\mathcal{C}_{t,i_{1},\dots,i_{K}})^{2}\\
&=\frac{1}{Td}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\|\widehat{\mathbf{c}}_{t}-\mathbf{c}_{t}\|^{2}=\frac{1}{Td}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\|\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{\otimes}\widehat{\mathbf{f}}_{Z,t}-\mathbf{Q}_{\otimes}\mathbf{f}_{Z,t}\|^{2}\\
&=\frac{1}{Td}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\bigg\|\big(\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{\otimes}-\mathbf{Q}_{\otimes}\mathbf{H}_{\otimes}^{\prime}\big)(\mathbf{H}_{\otimes}^{\prime})^{-1}\mathbf{f}_{Z,t}+\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{\otimes}\bigg(\widetilde{\bm{\varepsilon}}_{H,t}+\widetilde{\bm{\varepsilon}}_{t}+\widetilde{\mathbf{f}}_{Z,t}\bigg)\bigg\|^{2}\\
&=O_{P}\Bigg(\max_{k\in[K]}\Bigg\{T^{-1}d_{k}^{3\alpha_{k,1}-2\alpha_{k,r_{k}}}g_{s}^{-1}+dg_{s}^{-1}d_{k}^{2\alpha_{k,1}-3\alpha_{k,r_{k}}-1}+d^{-1}g_{\eta}g_{s}d_{k}^{2\alpha_{k,1}-3\alpha_{k,r_{k}}+1}\Bigg\}+\frac{1}{g_{w}}\Bigg),\end{split}

(6.65)

where we used (
6.62
), (
6.64
), (
6.63
) and Lemma
6
in the last equality, and the fact that
\|\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{\otimes}\|_{F}^{2}=r=O(1)
. This completes the proof of Theorem
2
.

\square

Proof of Theorem
5
.
Firstly, we use the notations in (
6.2
), and define also
\mathbf{Z}:=\mathbf{Z}_{k}
and
\mathbf{R}^{*}:=\mathbf{R}_{k}^{*}
, which coincides with the
\mathbf{R}^{*}
defined in (
6.20
). Then for
j\in[r_{k}]
,

\displaystyle\lambda_{j}(\mathbf{R}^{*})

\displaystyle=\lambda_{j}\Bigg(\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{Q}\mathbf{F}_{Z,t}\mathbf{\Lambda}^{\prime}\mathbf{\Lambda}\mathbf{F}_{Z,t}^{\prime}\mathbf{Q}^{\prime}\Bigg)=\lambda_{j}\Bigg(\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{A}_{k}\mathbf{F}_{t}\big[\otimes_{j\in[K]\setminus\{k\}}\mathbf{A}_{j}\big]^{\prime}\big[\otimes_{j\in[K]\setminus\{k\}}\mathbf{A}_{j}\big]\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{k}^{\prime}\Bigg)

\displaystyle=\lambda_{j}\Bigg(\mathbf{A}_{k}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{k}\cdot\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}[\otimes_{\ell\in[K]\setminus\{k\}}\mathbf{A}_{\ell}]^{\prime}[\otimes_{\ell\in[K]\setminus\{k\}}\mathbf{A}_{\ell}]\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\Bigg)

\displaystyle\asymp_{P}\lambda_{j}(\mathbf{A}_{k}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{k}\cdot\textnormal{tr}(\otimes_{\ell\in[K]\setminus\{k\}}\mathbf{A}_{\ell}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{\ell}))=\lambda_{j}(\mathbf{A}_{k}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{k})\prod_{\ell\in[K]\setminus\{k\}}\textnormal{tr}(\mathbf{A}_{\ell}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{\ell})

\displaystyle\asymp\lambda_{j}(\mathbf{Z}\mathbf{Q}^{\prime}\mathbf{Q})\prod_{\ell\in[K]\setminus\{k\}}\sum_{i=1}^{r_{k}}d_{\ell}^{\alpha_{\ell,i}}\asymp\lambda_{j}(\bm{\Sigma}_{A,k}^{1/2}\mathbf{Z}\bm{\Sigma}_{A,k}^{1/2})\cdot\prod_{\ell\in[K]\setminus\{k\}}d_{\ell}^{\alpha_{\ell,1}}

\displaystyle\asymp\lambda_{j}(\mathbf{Z})d_{k}^{-\alpha_{k,1}}g_{s}\asymp g_{s}d_{k}^{\alpha_{k,j}-\alpha_{k,1}},

(6.66)

where the third line uses Assumption (F1), and Assumption (L1) in the second last line. The last line uses Theorem 1 of
Ostrowski, 1959
on the eigenvalues of a congruent transformation
\bm{\Sigma}_{A,k}^{1/2}\mathbf{Z}\bm{\Sigma}_{A,k}^{1/2}
of
\mathbf{Z}
, and from Assumption (L1) that
\bm{\Sigma}_{A,k}
has eigenvalues uniformly bounded away from 0 and infinity.

Since
\widehat{\mathbf{S}}=\mathbf{R}^{*}+(\widehat{\mathbf{S}}-\mathbf{R}^{*})
, for
j\in[r_{k}]
, we have by Weyl’s inequality that

\displaystyle|\lambda_{j}(\widehat{\mathbf{S}})-\lambda_{j}(\mathbf{R}^{*})|

\displaystyle\leq\big\|\widehat{\mathbf{S}}-\mathbf{R}^{*}\big\|\leq\big\|\widetilde{\mathbf{R}}-\mathbf{R}^{*}\big\|+\big\|\mathbf{R}_{1}\big\|+\big\|\mathbf{R}_{2}\big\|+\big\|\mathbf{R}_{3}\big\|

\displaystyle\leq\omega_{k}\bigg\{\sup_{\|\bm{\gamma}\|=1}|\widetilde{R}(\bm{\gamma})-R^{*}(\bm{\gamma})|+\sup_{\|\bm{\gamma}\|=1}R_{1}+\sup_{\|\bm{\gamma}\|=1}R_{2}+\sup_{\|\bm{\gamma}\|=1}R_{3}\bigg\}=o_{P}(\omega_{k}),

(6.67)

where we use the decomposition in (
6.20
) in the first line, and
\omega_{k}:=g_{s}d_{k}^{\alpha_{k,r_{k}}-\alpha_{k,1}}
is defined at the beginning of the proof of Lemma
2
.
The second line uses
\widetilde{R}(\bm{\gamma}),R^{*}(\bm{\gamma}),R_{1},R_{2}
and
R_{3}
defined in (
6.21
), and the convergence in probability in (
6.25
) and (
6.30
).

Secondly, with Assumption (R1) and our choice of
\xi
(see also (
6.23
)),

\displaystyle\xi/\omega_{k}\asymp dg_{s}^{-1}d_{k}^{\alpha_{k,1}-\alpha_{k,r_{k}}}[(Td_{\text{-}k})^{-1/2}+d_{k}^{-1/2}]=o(1).

(6.68)

For
r_{k}>1
, if
j\in[r_{k}-1]
, using (
6.67
) and (
6.68
), consider

\displaystyle\frac{\lambda_{j+1}(\widehat{\mathbf{S}})+\xi}{\lambda_{j}(\widehat{\mathbf{S}})+\xi}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\lambda_{j+1}(\mathbf{R}^{*})+\xi+|\lambda_{j+1}(\widehat{\mathbf{S}})-\lambda_{j+1}(\mathbf{R}^{*})|}{\lambda_{j}(\mathbf{R}^{*})+\xi-|\lambda_{j}(\widehat{\mathbf{S}})-\lambda_{j}(\mathbf{R}^{*})|}=\frac{\lambda_{j+1}(\mathbf{R}^{*})+o_{P}(\omega_{k})}{\lambda_{j}(\mathbf{R}^{*})+o_{P}(\omega_{k})}

\displaystyle=\frac{\lambda_{j+1}(\mathbf{R}^{*})}{\lambda_{j}(\mathbf{R}^{*})}(1+o_{P}(1))\asymp_{P}d_{k}^{\alpha_{k,j+1}-\alpha_{k,j}},

(6.69)

where the last line uses (
6.66
). Also, for
j\in[r_{k}-1]
,

\displaystyle\frac{\lambda_{r_{k}+1}(\widehat{\mathbf{S}})+\xi}{\lambda_{r_{k}}(\widehat{\mathbf{S}})+\xi}

\displaystyle=\frac{\lambda_{r_{k}+1}(\widehat{\mathbf{S}})+\xi}{\omega_{k}(1+o_{P}(1))}=O_{P}\bigg(\frac{\lambda_{r_{k}+1}(\widehat{\mathbf{S}})}{\omega_{k}}+\frac{\xi}{\omega_{k}}\bigg)

(6.70)

\displaystyle=O_{P}\Big(\sup_{\|\bm{\gamma}\|=1}(\widetilde{R}(\bm{\gamma})-R^{*}(\bm{\gamma})+R_{1}+R_{2}+R_{3})+\xi/\omega_{k}\Big)

\displaystyle=O_{P}(\xi/\omega_{k})=o_{P}(d_{k}^{\alpha_{k,j+1}-\alpha_{k,j}}),

(6.71)

where the second last equality uses (
6.23
), (
6.24
) and (
6.30
) together with our choice of
\xi
,
and the last equality uses the extra rate assumption in the statement of the theorem.
In the third equality, we assume the following is true (to be shown at the end of this proof):

\lambda_{j}(\widehat{\mathbf{S}})=\lambda_{j}((\widetilde{\mathbf{R}}-\mathbf{R}^{*})+\mathbf{R}_{1}+\mathbf{R}_{2}+\mathbf{R}_{3}),\;\;\;j=r_{k}+1,\ldots,d_{k},

(6.72)

so that

\displaystyle\frac{\lambda_{j}(\widehat{\mathbf{S}})}{\omega_{k}}=\lambda_{j}\bigg(\frac{1}{\omega_{k}}((\widetilde{\mathbf{R}}-\mathbf{R}^{*})+\mathbf{R}_{1}+\mathbf{R}_{2}+\mathbf{R}_{3})\bigg)\leq\sup_{\|\bm{\gamma}\|=1}((\widetilde{R}(\bm{\gamma})-R^{*}(\bm{\gamma}))+R_{1}+R_{2}+R_{3}).

Hence for
j=r_{k}+1,\ldots,\lfloor d_{k}/2\rfloor
(true also for
r_{k}=1
),

\displaystyle\frac{\lambda_{j+1}(\widehat{\mathbf{S}})+\xi}{\lambda_{j}(\widehat{\mathbf{S}})+\xi}\geq\frac{\xi/\omega_{k}}{\sup_{\|\bm{\gamma}\|=1}((\widetilde{R}(\bm{\gamma})-R^{*}(\bm{\gamma}))+R_{1}+R_{2}+R_{3})+\xi/\omega_{k}}\geq\frac{1}{C}

(6.73)

in probability for some generic constant
C>0
, where the last inequality uses (
6.23
), (
6.24
) and (
6.30
) together with our choice of
\xi
.
Combining (
6.69
), (
6.71
) and (
6.73
), we can easily see that our proposed
\widehat{r}_{k}
is a consistent estimator for
r_{k}
.

If
r_{k}=1
, then (
6.71
) becomes

\frac{\lambda_{r_{k}+1}(\widehat{\mathbf{S}})+\xi}{\lambda_{r_{k}}(\widehat{\mathbf{S}})+\xi}=O_{P}(\xi/\omega_{k})=o_{P}(1).

When combined with (
6.73
) which is true also for
r_{k}=1
, we can see that
\widehat{r}_{k}=1
in probability, showing that
\widehat{r}_{k}
is a consistent estimator of
r_{k}
.

It remains to show (
6.72
). To this end, from (
6.66
) and (
6.67
), the first
r_{k}
eigenvalues of
\widehat{\mathbf{S}}
coincides with those of
\mathbf{R}^{*}
asymptotically, so that the first
r_{k}
eigenvectors corresponding to
\widehat{\mathbf{S}}
coincides with those for
\mathbf{R}^{*}
asymptotically as
T,d_{k}\rightarrow\infty
, which are necessarily in
\mathcal{N}^{\perp}:=\text{Span}(\mathbf{Q})
, the linear span of the columns of
\mathbf{Q}
(see (
4.5
), where
\mathbf{R}^{*}
is sandwiched by
\mathbf{Q}
and
\mathbf{Q}^{\prime}
). This means that the
(r_{k}+1)
-th largest eigenvalue of
\widehat{\mathbf{S}}
and beyond will asymptotically have eigenvectors in
\mathcal{N}
, the orthogonal complement of
\mathcal{N}^{\perp}
. Then for any unit vectors
\bm{\gamma}\in\mathcal{N}
, we have from the definitions of
\mathbf{R}^{*}
,
\widetilde{\mathbf{R}}
.
\mathbf{R}_{1}
,
\mathbf{R}_{2}
and
\mathbf{R}_{3}
in (
6.21
) that

\displaystyle\bm{\gamma}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{S}}\bm{\gamma}=\bm{\gamma}^{\prime}(\mathbf{R}^{*}+(\widetilde{\mathbf{R}}-\mathbf{R}^{*})+\mathbf{R}_{1}+\mathbf{R}_{2}+\mathbf{R}_{3})\bm{\gamma}=\bm{\gamma}^{\prime}((\widetilde{\mathbf{R}}-\mathbf{R}^{*})+\mathbf{R}_{1}+\mathbf{R}_{2}+\mathbf{R}_{3})\bm{\gamma},

which is equivalent to (
6.72
). This completes the proof of the theorem.
\square

Proof of Proposition
1
.

We can show stable convergence in law similar to Proposition 3.1 in
Xiong and Pelger, 2023
. First, using Assumption (F1) we can write

\begin{split}&\hskip 14.22636pt\sqrt{Td_{k}^{\alpha_{k,r_{k}}}}\cdot\mathbf{D}^{-1}\mathbf{H}_{k}^{a,\ast}\sum_{i=1}^{d_{k}}\mathbf{Q}_{k,i\cdot}\mathbf{A}_{k,i\cdot}^{\prime}\sum_{h=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}\Delta_{F,k,ij,h}\mathbf{A}_{k,j\cdot}\\
&=\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sqrt{\frac{{d_{k}^{\alpha_{k,r_{k}}}}}{T}}\cdot\sum_{i=1}^{d_{k}}\sum_{h=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}\Bigg(\frac{T\cdot\mathbbm{1}\{t\in\psi_{k,ij,h}\}}{|\psi_{k,ij,h}|}-1\Bigg)\\
&\cdot\mathbf{D}^{-1}\mathbf{H}_{k}^{a,\ast}\mathbf{Q}_{k,i\cdot}\mathbf{A}_{k,i\cdot}^{\prime}(\textnormal{mat}_{k}(\mathcal{F}_{t})\mathbf{v}_{k,h}\mathbf{v}_{k,h}^{\prime}\textnormal{mat}_{k}(\mathcal{F}_{t})^{\prime}-\mathbf{v}_{k,h}^{\prime}\mathbf{v}_{k,h}\bm{\Sigma}_{k})\mathbf{A}_{k,j\cdot}.\end{split}

Define the filtration
\mathcal{G}^{T}:=\sigma(\cup_{s=1}^{T}\mathcal{G}_{s})
where the sigma-algebra
\mathcal{G}_{s}:=\sigma(\{\mathcal{M}_{t,i_{1},\dots,i_{K}}\mid t\leq s\},\mathbf{A}_{1},\dots,\mathbf{A}_{K})
. Let
\mathbf{u}\in\mathbbm{R}^{r_{k}}
be a non-random unit vector. For a given
k\in[K],j\in[d_{k}]
, define also the random variable

\begin{split}g_{k,j,t}&:=\mathbf{u}^{\prime}\sqrt{\frac{{d_{k}^{\alpha_{k,r_{k}}}}}{T}}\cdot\sum_{i=1}^{d_{k}}\sum_{h=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}\Bigg(\frac{T\cdot\mathbbm{1}\{t\in\psi_{k,ij,h}\}}{|\psi_{k,ij,h}|}-1\Bigg)\\
&\cdot\mathbf{D}^{-1}\mathbf{H}_{k}^{a,\ast}\mathbf{Q}_{k,i\cdot}\mathbf{A}_{k,i\cdot}^{\prime}(\textnormal{mat}_{k}(\mathcal{F}_{t})\mathbf{v}_{k,h}\mathbf{v}_{k,h}^{\prime}\textnormal{mat}_{k}(\mathcal{F}_{t})^{\prime}-\mathbf{v}_{k,h}^{\prime}\mathbf{v}_{k,h}\bm{\Sigma}_{k})\mathbf{A}_{k,j\cdot}.\end{split}

Since each entry in
\mathcal{F}_{t}
is i.i.d. by Assumption (F1) and is independent of
(\mathcal{M}_{t},\mathbf{A}_{1},\dots,\mathbf{A}_{K})
by Assumptions (O1) and (L1), we have
\mathbbm{E}[g_{k,j,t}\mid\mathcal{G}_{t-1}]=0
. Define
\mathbf{\Xi}_{F,k}:=\text{var}\Big[\vec{\textnormal{mat}_{k}(\mathcal{F}_{t})\mathbf{A}_{\text{-}k}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{\text{-}k}\textnormal{mat}_{k}(\mathcal{F}_{t})^{\prime}-\textnormal{tr}(\mathbf{A}_{\text{-}k}\mathbf{A}_{\text{-}k}^{\prime})\bm{\Sigma}_{k}}\Big]
and
\mathbf{x}_{F,k,j,il}:=\vec{[\mathbf{A}_{k,j\cdot}^{\prime}\otimes(\mathbf{D}^{-1}\mathbf{H}_{k}^{a,\ast}\mathbf{Q}_{k,i\cdot}\mathbf{A}_{k,i\cdot}^{\prime})]\mathbf{\Xi}_{F,k}[\mathbf{A}_{k,j\cdot}^{\prime}\otimes(\mathbf{D}^{-1}\mathbf{H}_{k}^{a,\ast}\mathbf{Q}_{k,l\cdot}\mathbf{A}_{k,l\cdot}^{\prime})]^{\prime}}
, so that we have

\|\mathbf{x}_{F,k,j,il}\|^{2}\leq\|\mathbf{\Xi}_{F,k}\|_{F}^{2}\cdot\|\mathbf{D}^{-1}\|_{F}^{4}\cdot\|\mathbf{Z}_{k}^{-1/2}\|_{F}^{4}=O_{P}\Bigg(d_{k}^{4\alpha_{k,1}-6\alpha_{k,r_{k}}}\prod_{j=1}^{K}d_{j}^{-4\alpha_{j,1}}\prod_{j\in[K]\setminus\{k\}}d_{j}^{4\alpha_{j,1}}\Bigg)=O_{P}(d_{k}^{-6\alpha_{k,r_{k}}}),

leading to

\displaystyle d_{k}^{\alpha_{k,r_{k}}}\cdot\mathbbm{E}\Bigg\|\sum_{i=1}^{d_{k}}\sum_{l=1}^{d_{k}}\Bigg(\frac{T\cdot\mathbbm{1}\{t\in\psi_{k,ij}\}}{|\psi_{k,ij}|}-1\Bigg)\Bigg(\frac{T\cdot\mathbbm{1}\{t\in\psi_{k,lj}\}}{|\psi_{k,lj}|}-1\Bigg)(\mathbf{x}_{F,k,j,il}-\mathbbm{E}[\mathbf{x}_{F,k,j,il}])\Bigg\|^{2}

\displaystyle=O_{P}(d_{k}^{4-5\alpha_{k,r_{k}}})=o_{P}(1).

Hence, it holds that

\begin{split}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbbm{E}[g_{k,j,t}^{2}\mid\mathcal{G}_{t-1}]&=\frac{d_{k}^{\alpha_{k,r_{k}}}}{T}\cdot\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbbm{E}\Bigg\{\mathbf{u}^{\prime}\sum_{i=1}^{d_{k}}\sum_{l=1}^{d_{k}}\Bigg(\frac{T\cdot\mathbbm{1}\{t\in\psi_{k,ij}\}}{|\psi_{k,ij}|}-1\Bigg)\Bigg(\frac{T\cdot\mathbbm{1}\{t\in\psi_{k,lj}\}}{|\psi_{k,lj}|}-1\Bigg)\\
&\cdot\mathbf{D}^{-1}\mathbf{H}_{k}^{a,\ast}[\mathbf{A}_{k,j\cdot}^{\prime}\otimes(\mathbf{Q}_{k,i\cdot}\mathbf{A}_{k,i\cdot}^{\prime})]\vec{\textnormal{mat}_{k}(\mathcal{F}_{t})\mathbf{A}_{\text{-}k}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{\text{-}k}\textnormal{mat}_{k}(\mathcal{F}_{t})^{\prime}-\textnormal{tr}(\mathbf{A}_{\text{-}k}\mathbf{A}_{\text{-}k}^{\prime})\bm{\Sigma}_{k}}\\
&\cdot\vec{\textnormal{mat}_{k}(\mathcal{F}_{t})\mathbf{A}_{\text{-}k}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{\text{-}k}\textnormal{mat}_{k}(\mathcal{F}_{t})^{\prime}-\textnormal{tr}(\mathbf{A}_{\text{-}k}\mathbf{A}_{\text{-}k}^{\prime})\bm{\Sigma}_{k}}^{\prime}[\mathbf{A}_{k,j\cdot}^{\prime}\otimes(\mathbf{Q}_{k,l\cdot}\mathbf{A}_{k,l\cdot}^{\prime})]^{\prime}(\mathbf{H}_{k}^{a,\ast})^{\prime}\mathbf{D}^{-1}\mathbf{u}\mid\mathcal{G}_{t-1}\Bigg\}\\
&\xrightarrow{p}d_{k}^{2+\alpha_{k,r_{k}}}\cdot\lim_{d_{k}\to\infty}\frac{1}{d_{k}^{2}}\sum_{i=1}^{d_{k}}\sum_{l=1}^{d_{k}}\Bigg(\frac{T\cdot\mathbbm{1}\{t\in\psi_{k,ij}\}}{|\psi_{k,ij}|}-1\Bigg)\Bigg(\frac{T\cdot\mathbbm{1}\{t\in\psi_{k,lj}\}}{|\psi_{k,lj}|}-1\Bigg)\\
&\cdot\mathbf{u}^{\prime}\mathbf{D}^{-1}\mathbf{H}_{k}^{a,\ast}[\mathbf{A}_{k,j\cdot}^{\prime}\otimes(\mathbf{Q}_{k}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{k})]\mathbf{\Xi}_{F,k}[\mathbf{A}_{k,j\cdot}^{\prime}\otimes(\mathbf{Q}_{k}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{k})]^{\prime}(\mathbf{H}_{k}^{a,\ast})^{\prime}\mathbf{D}^{-1}\mathbf{u}\\
&\xrightarrow{p}d_{k}^{2+\alpha_{k,r_{k}}}\omega_{\psi,k,j}\cdot\mathbf{u}^{\prime}\mathbf{D}^{-1}\mathbf{H}_{k}^{a,\ast}[\mathbf{A}_{k,j\cdot}^{\prime}\otimes(\mathbf{Q}_{k}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{k})]\mathbf{\Xi}_{F,k}[\mathbf{A}_{k,j\cdot}^{\prime}\otimes(\mathbf{Q}_{k}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{k})]^{\prime}(\mathbf{H}_{k}^{a,\ast})^{\prime}\mathbf{D}^{-1}\mathbf{u},\end{split}

which satisfies the nesting condition of Theorem 6.1 in
Häusler and Luschgy, 2015
. From Assumption (O1), we have
|(T\cdot\mathbbm{1}\{t\in\psi_{k,ij,h}\}/|\psi_{k,ij,h}|)-1|\leq\max(\psi_{0}^{-1}-1,1)
. Hence with
\epsilon
from Proposition
1
, we have

\begin{split}&\hskip 14.22636pt\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbbm{E}[g_{k,j,t}^{2+\epsilon}\mid\mathcal{G}_{t-1}]\leq\|\mathbf{A}_{k,j\cdot}\|^{2+\epsilon}\cdot\frac{d_{k}^{\alpha_{k,r_{k}}(1+\epsilon/2)}}{T^{1+\epsilon/2}}\cdot\|\mathbf{D}^{-1}\mathbf{H}_{k}^{a,\ast}\|^{2+\epsilon}\\
&\cdot\sum_{t=1}^{T}\Bigg\|\sum_{i=1}^{d_{k}}\sum_{h=1}^{d_{\text{-}k}}\Bigg(\frac{T\cdot\mathbbm{1}\{t\in\psi_{k,ij,h}\}}{|\psi_{k,ij,h}|}-1\Bigg)\mathbf{Q}_{k,i\cdot}\mathbf{A}_{k,i\cdot}^{\prime}(\textnormal{mat}_{k}(\mathcal{F}_{t})\mathbf{v}_{k,h}\mathbf{v}_{k,h}^{\prime}\textnormal{mat}_{k}(\mathcal{F}_{t})^{\prime}-\mathbf{v}_{k,h}^{\prime}\mathbf{v}_{k,h}\bm{\Sigma}_{k})\Bigg\|^{2+\epsilon}\\
&=O_{P}(1)\cdot(d_{k}^{\alpha_{k,1}-\alpha_{k,r_{k}}}g_{s}^{-1})^{2+\epsilon}\cdot\frac{d_{k}^{\alpha_{k,r_{k}}(1+\epsilon/2)}}{T^{\epsilon/2}}\cdot d_{k}^{-\alpha_{k,r_{k}}(1+\epsilon/2)}g_{s}^{2+\epsilon}=O_{P}\Big(\frac{d_{k}^{\alpha_{k,1}-\alpha_{k,r_{k}}}}{T^{\epsilon/2}}\Big)=o_{P}(1),\end{split}

which is sufficient for the conditional Lindeberg condition in
Häusler and Luschgy, 2015
to hold. Then by the stable martingale central limit theorem (Theorem 6.1 in
Häusler and Luschgy, 2015
), we have

\sum_{t=1}^{T}g_{k,j,t}\to\mathcal{N}(0,\mathbf{D}^{-1}\mathbf{H}_{k}^{a,\ast}h_{k,j}(\mathbf{A}_{k,j\cdot})(\mathbf{H}_{k}^{a,\ast})^{\prime}\mathbf{D}^{-1})\;\;\;\text{$\mathcal{G}^{T}$-stably as }T\to\infty,

where
h_{k,j}(\mathbf{A}_{k,j\cdot})=d_{k}^{2+\alpha_{k,r_{k}}}\omega_{\psi,k,j}\cdot[\mathbf{A}_{k,j\cdot}^{\prime}\otimes(\mathbf{Q}_{k}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{k})]\mathbf{\Xi}_{F,k}[\mathbf{A}_{k,j\cdot}^{\prime}\otimes(\mathbf{Q}_{k}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{k})]^{\prime}
. This completes the proof of Proposition
1
.

\square
